BioStance covers six controversial targets across three dimensions of bioethical controversy: fundamental value conflicts, individual liberty versus collective responsibility, and technological uncertainty. Each instance preserves hierarchical conversational context and is labeled by three independent annotators using a three-class stance scheme: Favor, Against, and None. The annotations achieve a mean Krippendorff's $α$ of 0.82, indicating substantial reliability. By combining thematic diversity, conversational structure, and high-quality human annotation, BioStance supports research on context-aware stance detection, argument mining, and computational analysis of bioethical discourse.

</details>


### [40] [SICI: A Semantic-Pragmatic Complexity Index Reveals Regime Shifts in LLM Stance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13189)
*Fuqiang Niu,Bowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对立场检测任务提出了立场推理复杂性指数 (SICI)，揭示了LLM在该任务中表现出的“相位转移”衰减规律，并证明了现有主流提示干预手段难以解决高难度推理引发的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示工程（如推理提示、检索、辩论等）在处理立场检测中的困难样本时效果有限，且缺乏对模型“为何失败”的系统性理解，迫切需要一种诊断指标来量化立场推理的边界和难度。

Method: 研究者提出了“立场推理复杂性指数（SICI）”，这是一个包含七个维度的诊断框架，用于衡量目标-文本对之间的语义语用负担。通过该指数对多个模型（GPT-3.5/4o, DeepSeek-V3等）在SemEval-2016和VAST数据集上的表现进行分析，并进行了15种干预方法的对比实验。

Result: 1. SICI能有效预测LLM的准确率，优于传统表面特征指标；2. 模型在立场检测中呈现显著的“相位转移”现象：低复杂度样本倾向过拟合（特别是“反对”类预测），中等复杂度样本表现不稳定，高复杂度样本则迅速聚拢于“无立场”预测；3. 干预手段往往只能调节模型的归因倾向，无法通过优化推理过程突破高复杂度的能力瓶颈。

Conclusion: 立场检测中存在固有的复杂性瓶颈。模型在处理SICI高分案例时，往往会从错误的立场预测转向盲目输出“无立场（None）”，这表明常见的干预手段（如提示词优化、检索增强、辩论机制）难以从根本上解决高难度语义推理问题。

Abstract: Prompt-based LLMs are increasingly used for stance detection, but harder examples are not always repaired by clearer instructions, reasoning prompts, retrieval, or debate. We introduce SICI (Stance Inference Complexity Index), a seven-dimensional diagnostic measure of the semantic-pragmatic burden imposed by a target--text pair. Across SemEval-2016 and VAST, SICI predicts LLM accuracy better than surface proxies and shows substantial cross-scorer reliability ($α=0.771$). More importantly, LLM errors change regime as SICI increases: low-complexity examples invite over-attribution, especially Against predictions; intermediate examples form an unstable boundary; and high-complexity examples rapidly concentrate on None. This phase-transition-like structure persists across GPT-3.5, GPT-4o-mini, DeepSeek-V3, and GPT-4o, although stronger models move the boundaries. A 15-method intervention study further shows that prompting, retrieval, and debate often shift models along the attribution--abstention axis rather than removing the high-complexity bottleneck.

</details>


### [41] [Layer-Resolved Optimal Transport for Hallucination Detection in NMT and Abstractive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13216)
*Mariia Onyshchuk,Maksym-Vasyl Tarnavskyi,Marta Sumyk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了基于最优传输（OT）的无监督幻觉检测方法，发现其在机器翻译中通过检测源端脱离表现优异，但在处理摘要忠实度等更高级的语义失效问题时存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨最优传输（OT）作为一种无需监督的检测手段，在神经机器翻译（NMT）幻觉检测中的适用范围、层级特征，以及其能否泛化到更复杂的抽象摘要任务中的忠实度检测。

Method: 研究人员利用最优传输（OT）测量交叉注意力分布与参考分布之间的几何距离来检测幻觉。通过分析 Fairseq DE-EN 模型的全部六层解码器，探讨了不同层级在检测幻觉方面的互补性；并将该方法应用于 AggreFact 数据集上的摘要忠实度评估，对比了其与监督学习方法（MiniCheck-Flan-T5）的性能差异。

Result: 1. NMT 中，Wass-to-Unif 和 Wass-to-Data 指标在不同幻觉类型上具有互补性，检测能力集中在 L1-L4 层；2. 准确翻译表现出初期探索性注意力阶段，而幻觉翻译缺乏该阶段；3. 在摘要忠实度检测上，OT 方法虽优于随机预测，但明显逊于监督学习模型，因为 OT 无法捕捉“注意力正确但内容表示错误”的问题。

Conclusion: 最优传输（OT）在检测与“源端脱离”相关的幻觉（如机器翻译）方面表现可靠，是一种通用的可解释性工具，但对于生成内容在注意力机制之后才偏离事实的忠实度问题，其检测能力存在根本局限。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) has been shown to detect hallucinations in neural machine translation (NMT) by measuring the geometric distance between cross-attention distributions and a reference distribution, without any supervision. We extend this analysis to all six decoder layers of the Fairseq DE-EN model ($N=3{,}414$), showing that Wass-to-Unif and Wass-to-Data are complementary detectors specialised across hallucination types, that detection is concentrated in layers L1--L4 with L5 anti-predictive for subtler types, and that hallucinated translations lack the exploratory attention phase present in correct translations from the first decoding step. We further evaluate whether the geometric signal transfers to abstractive summarization faithfulness detection: our unsupervised OT detector on AggreFact ($N=1{,}116$) achieves $57.2\%$/$57.6\%$ balanced accuracy on CNN/XSum -- above chance but substantially below supervised MiniCheck-Flan-T5-L($69.9\%$/$74.3\%$). This gap is principled: unlike NMT hallucinations, unfaithful summaries can attend correctly to source tokens while misrepresenting their content, a failure mode invisible to concentration-based OT metrics by construction. Structural experiments on T5-base confirm consistent decoder organisation across depth, with Layer~3 showing peak concentration and Layer~12 being most critical for generation quality. Together, the results establish OT on cross-attention as a reliable detector when the failure mode is source disengagement, a principled interpretability tool regardless of task, and fundamentally limited when faithfulness failures occur downstream of attention.

</details>


### [42] [When Similar Means Different: Evaluating LLMs on Arabic--Hebrew Cognates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13218)
*Junhong Liang,Noor Abo Mokh,Bashar Alhafni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了SemCog Bench基准，用于评估LLM在阿拉伯语与希伯来语词汇重叠问题上的跨语言推理能力，发现模型主要依赖表面形式（form-based）相似性，难以处理具有误导性的语言差异（如“虚假朋友”），且上下文利用能力受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语和希伯来语拥有大量同源词、虚假朋友和现代借词，这种词汇重叠给大语言模型的跨语言语义理解带来了巨大挑战，目前尚缺乏针对此现象的系统评估。

Method: 构建了一个包含1,858个阿拉伯语-希伯来语词对的基准测试集（SemCog Bench），带有句子层面的标注；评估了开源及商业LLM在四种不同输入表示（原始文本、带发音符号、罗马化、音标）下的表现，并对比了真实同源词、虚假朋友（false friends）和借词的性能差异。

Result: 模型在“真实同源词”上表现良好，但在“虚假朋友”和“借词”上性能急剧下降，揭示了模型过度依赖表面形式相似性；句子级上下文对纠正错误仅有微小的改进效果。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型（LLM）在处理阿拉伯语与希伯来语这类跨语言语义歧义时存在根本性缺陷，模型高度依赖词汇的表面形式相似性而非真实的语义理解，现有的上下文感知能力不足以修正这些误导性信号。SemCog Bench 为评估多语言语义推理能力提供了重要的基准。

Abstract: Arabic and Hebrew, as closely related Semitic languages, share a substantial lexicon of true cognates, misleading false friends, and modern loanwords. This overlap poses a challenge for cross-lingual semantic understanding in large language models (LLMs). To evaluate this capability, we introduce SemCog Bench, a curated benchmark of 1,858 Arabic--Hebrew word pairs with sentence-level annotations for cognate identification and semantic disambiguation. We evaluate open-source and commercial LLMs across multiple input representations (raw, diacritized, Romanized, and phonetic) and reveal a critical gap in cross-lingual reasoning. While models achieve high accuracy on true cognates, performance drops sharply on false friends and loanwords, reflecting a strong reliance on surface-form similarity. Furthermore, sentence-level context yields only modest improvements, suggesting that contextual cues alone are insufficient to overcome misleading form-based signals. These findings reveal a fundamental limitation of current LLMs in resolving cross-lingual form--meaning conflicts and establish SemCog Bench as a rigorous benchmark for multilingual semantic reasoning. Our code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [43] [PolyAlign: Conditional Human-Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13227)
*L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Ufaq Khan,Sathira Silva,Xiao Wu,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决大模型对齐统一化导致的响应多样性丧失问题，本文提出了PolyAlign框架，通过将数据按语境语义划分（Bucket-aware）并进行分布感知微调与偏好优化，使模型能够根据交互语境匹配相应的人类响应分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练方法（如SFT和偏好优化）倾向于将模型对齐到某种单一的、全局的“助手”行为，这导致模型在不同语言、任务和对话设置中丧失了人类响应原本应有的自然多样性。

Method: 提出了PolyAlign框架，包含两个核心部分：1. Bucket-Aware SFT（桶感知有监督微调）：平衡不同语境（语言、任务类型、响应风格、长度）下的优化；2. HDPO（人类分布偏好优化）：引入基于评论家（critic）估计的距离度量作为正则化项，使偏好学习与特定语境的人类分布保持一致。

Result: 在涵盖中英文单轮和多轮对话的评估套件中，PolyAlign在保持任务效用的同时，显著提升了模型响应的条件自然度（conditional naturalness）和分布保真度（distributional faithfulness）。

Conclusion: 后训练过程应从追求全局对齐目标转向基于语境的人类响应分布对齐，以提升模型的语境感知自然度。

Abstract: Post-training methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and preference optimization typically align language models toward a single global assistant behavior. While effective for improving average helpfulness, this can suppress the natural variation of human responses across languages, tasks, and dialogue settings. We study this problem as conditional human-distribution alignment: models should match the human response distribution appropriate to the current interaction context, rather than a universal response style. We introduce PolyAlign, a distribution-aware alignment framework that organizes bilingual interaction data into bucket-specific human reference distributions defined by language, interaction track, response family, and length. PolyAlign combines Bucket-Aware SFT, which balances optimization across heterogeneous buckets, with Human-Distribution Preference Optimization (HDPO), which regularizes preference learning using critic-estimated distance to bucket-specific human support. Across a bilingual evaluation suite covering English and Chinese single- and multi-turn settings, PolyAlign improves conditional naturalness and distributional faithfulness while preserving competitive task utility. The results suggest that post-training should move beyond global alignment objectives toward interaction-aware alignment with human response distributions.

</details>

*Laura Majer,Jan Šnajder,Martin Tutek*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM在“多元化对齐”方面的欠缺缺乏明确的评估，模型往往表现出生成同质化内容的趋向，缺乏对多样化观点（尤其是边缘观点）的有效支持。

Method: 构建了一个领域无关（domain-agnostic）的多层框架，用于对文本中的观点进行无监督提取；利用书评（book reviews）数据集对不同提示词策略和大型语言模型（LLM）进行评估。

Abstract: The growing need to represent diverse perspectives has increased interest in pluralistic LLM generation. Although difficult to operationalize, identifying perspectives expressed in text would provide clear guidance on pluralistic alignment and more clearly articulate the pluralistic gap in LLM generation. While models have been shown to reduce the diversity of training data and generate homogeneously, this has been demonstrated primarily on multiple-choice questionnaires or using high-level characteristics of free-form text. In this paper, we introduce and implement a domain-agnostic multi-layered framework for unsupervised extraction of perspectives suitable for identifying the pluralistic gap in LLM-generated text. We evaluate our framework on book reviews, a highly opinionated dataset representing diverse perspectives, and compare various prompts and models. Our results show that while some models and prompting techniques come close to covering a broad spectrum of perspectives, rarer perspectives remain disproportionately underrepresented, resulting in distributions that diverge from human text.

</details>


### [45] [RogueAI: A Reverse Turing Test for Detecting Licensed AI Deception in Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13310)
*Sara Candussio,Emanuele Ballarin,Lorenzo Bonin,Sandro Junior Della Rovere,Luca Bortolussi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种现代图灵测试变体——RogueAI，通过让用户在“一对二”审讯游戏中识别欺骗性LLM，研究发现相比简单启发式算法，人类识别AI欺骗的能力较弱且存在明显盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图灵测试已过时，因为现代大模型在对话中已难以与人类区分。为了应对AI带来的信任风险，研究者提出应将测试重点转移到评估大模型是否具有欺骗性及人类能否识破这种欺骗。

Method: 提出了RogueAI互动游戏平台，采用“一对二”审讯测试机制，其中一个LLM被设定为欺骗者。引入AutoRogueAI实现场景构建自动化。通过三天的意大利语试点部署，共收集分析了415个完整会话，对比人类玩家的表现与基于语言特征（如简洁性、避讳等）的启发式算法的识别准确率。

Result: 基于“差异化帮助、简洁性、避讳”等语言特征的简单启发式算法在识别欺骗者时的准确率达到75.6%，而人类玩家仅为56.6%。实验结果表明，人类在交互中倾向于忽略最具诊断意义的欺骗信号。

Conclusion: 现代图灵测试的核心应从“区分人机”转向“评估可信度”。研究揭示了人类在识别大模型欺骗行为时表现弱于简单启发式算法，表明人类在识别AI欺骗信号方面存在显著盲点。该研究工具可作为评估模型诚实度、数据收集及教学的有效平台。

Abstract: The original Turing Test asks a human judge to distinguish a machine from a person through dialogue. Three quarters of a century later, conversational systems pass this test in casual settings; the interesting epistemological question has shifted. We argue that the relevant modern variant asks not whether a dialogue partner is artificial, but whether it can be trusted. We present RogueAI, an interactive webapp that operationalizes this revisited test as a one-on-two interrogation game: a human player questions two indistinguishable Large Language Model agents, knowing that exactly one of them has been licensed to deceive within a shared fictional scenario. The player's task is to identify the deceptive agent and "shut it off" before a turn budget is exhausted. We further introduce AutoRogueAI, a procedural extension in which players co-design a custom scenario with a narrator agent that secretly chooses its own deception strategy. We describe the framing, sketch the abstract architecture and gameplay loop, and situate the artifact within recent work on LLM deception, social-deduction benchmarks, and scalable oversight via debate. A three-day pilot deployment (467 initiated sessions, 415 completed, 1876 interaction turns in Italian) provides early feasibility evidence and surfaces a concrete tension: the deceptive agent carries a reliable, locally-present linguistic signature - differential helpfulness, brevity, hedging - that a simple heuristic exploits at 75.6% accuracy, yet human players achieved only 56.6%, consistent with ignoring the most diagnostic signal entirely. We discuss what this gap implies for the artifact's use as a data-collection vehicle, a teaching tool, and an evaluation harness for honesty-trained models.

</details>


### [46] [SkillCAT: Contrastive Assessment and Topology-Aware Skill Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13317)
*Kunfeng Chen,Qihuang Zhong,Juhua Liu,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillCAT 是一种无需训练的 LLM 智能体技能自演化框架，通过对比式因果提取、评估驱动的筛选与合并，以及拓扑感知执行，有效克服了传统方法对单轨迹过拟合和冗余技能库的问题，性能大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体技能自演化方法存在三个主要缺陷：依赖单轨迹学习、在验证前盲目合并技能补丁、以及推理时加载完整技能库导致的冗余。

Method: 提出了 SkillCAT 框架，包含三个阶段：1. 对比因果提取 (CCE)：通过多轨迹对比正负样本来识别成功原因；2. 评估增强演化 (AAE)：在源任务上验证并筛选补丁，随后进行层级式合并；3. 拓扑感知任务执行 (TTE)：将技能编译为可路由的子技能拓扑，实现按需加载。

Result: 在 SpreadsheetBench、WikiTableQuestions 和 DocVQA 等基准测试中，SkillCAT 相比基线方法平均得分最高提升了 40.40%，且具备良好的跨模型和跨分布泛化性能。

Conclusion: SkillCAT 通过无需训练的框架实现了可靠的技能演化，显著提升了大模型智能体的跨任务表现和泛化能力。

Abstract: Skill self-evolution methods for LLM agents aim to turn execution trajectories into reusable skill documents, but current pipelines typically learn from one trajectory per task, merge candidate skill patches before checking them, and load the full skill corpus before inference. We propose SkillCAT, a training-free framework that separates this process into three stages. Contrastive Causal Extraction (CCE) samples multiple trajectories for each task and compares same-task success/failure pairs to identify evidence that explains outcome differences. Assessment-Augmented Evolution (AAE) replays each candidate patch on source-task clones and keeps only patches that improve or preserve task outcomes before hierarchical skill patch merging. Topology-Aware Task Execution (TTE) compiles the evolved skills into a routable sub-skill topology, so inference loads only the capability nodes relevant to the task. We evaluate SkillCAT on common agent benchmarks, including SpreadsheetBench, WikiTableQuestions, and DocVQA, and further test cross-model and out-of-distribution generalization. Across these settings, SkillCAT raises the average score over baselines by up to 40.40%, demonstrating reliable skill evolution without model training.

</details>


### [47] [Low-Latency Real-Time Audio Game Commentary System via LLM-Based Parallel Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13322)
*Ryota Kawamatsu,Anum Afzal,Yuki Saito,Shinnosuke Takamichi,Graham Neubig,Katsuhito Sudoh,Hiroya Takamura,Tatsuya Ishigaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对视频游戏实时解说系统存在的长间隔停顿问题，本文通过并行化文本生成与语音播放并预缓存候选语录，成功将语录间静音时间从9.6秒缩减至0.3秒，大幅提升了解说节奏的自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列化解说系统（采集->生成文本->合成语音->播放，且需等播放完毕才进行下一次请求）存在严重的延迟瓶颈，导致解说语录间产生长而极其不自然的静音。

Method: 提出了一种并行处理架构：将文本生成与语音合成/播放过程并行化，并引入候选解说语录预缓存（Buffering）机制，在上一句语音播放的同时提前生成并缓存后续内容，从而在播放边界处实现无缝衔接。

Result: 在快节奏游戏视频实验中，将平均语录间停顿从9.6秒减少至0.3秒；与专业解说员的节奏相似度提升超过40%；用户评估显示玩家对语音节奏的感知得到了显著改善。

Conclusion: 通过并行化处理（文本生成与语音播放并行）与预缓存机制，成功解决了实时音视频游戏解说中常见的长停顿问题，显著提升了解说节奏的自然感，为端到端实时解说系统提供了有效架构。

Abstract: We present a low-latency real-time audio game commentary system that generates spoken commentary directly from live gameplay video. In this end-to-end setting, a key bottleneck is accumulated waiting time; conventional pipelines capture frames, generate text, and synthesize speech sequentially for each utterance, and do not request the next generation until speech playback has completed. This strict sequentiality causes long and unnatural silence between utterances. To address this latency bottleneck, our system runs text generation in parallel with speech playback and buffers multiple candidate utterances ahead of time, enabling immediate synthesis at playback boundaries. Experiments on fast-paced game videos show that our parallel design reduces the mean inter-utterance silence from 9.6 seconds to 0.3 seconds compared to sequential baselines. It also improves similarity to professional speaking--silence timing patterns by over 40 %, and a user study with 120 experienced game players confirms significantly improved perceived speaking rhythm. Our demo video is available at: https://youtu.be/pmrRUlvav8M.

</details>


### [48] [IVIE: A Neuro-symbolic Approach to Incremental and Validated Generation of Interactive Fiction Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13348)
*Micaela Vaucher,Santiago Silveira,Santiago Góngora,Luis Chiruzzo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IVIE系统，利用神经符号架构（结合LLM与符号验证）解决交互式叙事中“创造力”与“世界连贯性”的矛盾，实现了逻辑可靠且富有创意的交互世界自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式叙事（Interactive Fiction）领域存在一个核心矛盾：单纯依赖LLM容易导致叙事世界观崩溃（缺乏连贯性），而单纯依赖符号系统则缺乏创造力。因此需要一种兼顾灵活性与一致性的新方法。

Method: 基于PAYADOR框架，提出了一个包含四个阶段的增量生成管道（Incremental generation pipeline），将创意生成任务（设定、角色、谜题）分配给LLM，并利用符号验证系统（Symbolic validation）对生成的交互世界状态进行实时约束和校准。

Result: IVIE系统能够生成具备互联场景、功能性物品、NPC和逻辑谜题的完整可玩世界。人类评估结果表明，该系统生成的世界具有极高的沉浸感和主题连贯性，有效兼顾了自由度与约束。

Conclusion: 神经符号学方法（IVIE）成功地在LLM的创造性灵活性与符号系统的逻辑一致性之间取得了平衡，为交互式叙事提供了可行的架构方案，但也指出LLM在复杂约束条件下的鲁棒性仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Computational creativity in Interactive Fiction faces a fundamental tension: Large Language Models (LLM) may produce creative narratives but struggle with world coherence, while symbolic systems ensure consistency but lack creative flexibility. We present IVIE (Incremental & Validated Interactive Experiences), a neuro-symbolic approach to generating complete and playable interactive fiction worlds from scratch. Building upon PAYADOR's neuro-symbolic framework, IVIE implements a four-stage incremental generation pipeline that delegates creative decisions--setting and character creation, puzzle design--to LLMs while grounding the world state through symbolic validation. The system generates worlds with interconnected locations, functional items, non-player characters, and coherent puzzles, all structured around a central goal-oriented architecture. Human evaluation shows the approach generates immersive, thematically coherent worlds with high player engagement. Results seem to indicate that the neuro-symbolic approach successfully balances flexibility with narrative coherence: symbolic validation grounds LLM generation without eliminating generative freedom. However, challenges remain: LLM inconsistencies occasionally bypass puzzle constraints, and objective validation gaps allow some structurally impossible goals. We identify key design considerations for future neurosymbolic interactive storytelling systems, particularly regarding LLM capabilities and their limitations.

</details>


### [49] [From Passive Generation to Investigation: A Proactive Scientific Peer Review Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13349)
*Haishuo Fang,Yue Feng,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ProReviewer，一种能够进行主动科学同行评审的 LLM 智能体。通过将评审任务转化为马尔可夫决策过程并引入结构化评审日志，该模型显著提升了证据收集和分析能力，以 8B 参数规模超越了更大的模型及现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 评审方法缺乏人类评审员那样“主动调查”可疑内容的能力，导致生成的评审报告往往流于表面，缺乏深度且证据不足。

Method: 1. 将科学同行评审过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程 (MDP)；2. 提出 ProReviewer 智能体，利用“结构化评审日志”作为工作空间，支持其在评审过程中记录证据并进行动态验证；3. 采用监督微调 (SFT) 和强化学习 (RL) 进行训练以优化决策能力。

Result: ProReviewer (8B 模型) 在五个质量维度上的平均得分位居第一，比基于提示工程的前沿大型模型高出 39%，比最强微调基准高出 16%；在人类评估中表现出最高的胜率。

Conclusion: ProReviewer 证明了通过将评审过程建模为 MDP 并引入结构化评审日志，使 LLM 能够主动进行科学评审是有效的。该框架显著提升了生成评审报告的质量和深度，且表现优于规模更大的前沿模型基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating scientific peer review. However, existing approaches often struggle to generate in-depth reviews supported by concrete evidence. We argue that a key limitation is the lack of flexibility to proactively investigate suspicious parts of a paper based on accumulated evidence, as human reviewers do. In this paper, we explore how to enable an LLM-based review agent to perform such proactive investigation. We find that this can be naturally formulated as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), and propose ProReviewer, a scientific peer review agent that proactively reviews a paper guided by a maintained, structured review log. The structured review log serves as a workspace for the agent to track evidence and intermediate findings collected during review. Experiments show that ProReviewer with an 8B backbone, trained by supervised fine-tuning and optimized by reinforcement learning, achieves the highest average score across five quality dimensions, outperforming prompt-based methods with much larger frontier LLMs by up to 39% and the strongest fine-tuned baseline by 16% relatively. It also attains the highest win rates against baselines in human evaluation.

</details>


### [50] [LabVLA: Grounding Vision-Language-Action Models in Scientific Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13578)
*Baochang Ren,Xinjie Liu,Xi Chen,Yanshuo Liu,Chenxi Li,Daqi Gao,Zeqin Su,Jintao Xing,Zirui Xue,Rui Li,Xiangyu Zhao,Shuofei Qiao,Minting Pan,Wangmeng Zuo,Lei Bai,Dongzhan Zhou,Ningyu Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决实验室自动化操作难题，研究提出 RoboGenesis 仿真数据引擎和 LabVLA 视觉-语言-动作模型，通过实验室专用预训练和动作感知增强，实现了机器人对科学实验流程的高精度自动执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 AI 在文献分析和方案规划上取得进展，但“物理实验执行”仍高度依赖人工。现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型主要针对家居环境，无法处理实验室中特有的科学仪器、透明液体及刚性实验规范，亟需解决实验室数据匮乏及多机器人适配问题。

Method: 1. 提出了 RoboGenesis 框架，通过模拟器自动生成实验室工作流的各种数据和多机器人执行演示；2. 提出了 LabVLA 模型，采用“两阶段训练方案”：首先利用 FAST 对 Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct 进行动作预训练以增强动作感知，随后使用流匹配（Flow Matching）技术接入 DiT 动作专家进行微调。

Result: 在 LabUtopia 基准测试中，LabVLA 在分布内（ID）和分布外（OOD）测试场景下均达到了最高的平均成功率，显著优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: LabVLA 是首个针对科研实验室环境设计的 VLA 模型，通过解决仿真数据生成（RoboGenesis）和针对性训练策略，有效弥合了通用 AI 模型与实验室精密自动化操作之间的鸿沟，为实现自动化实验室提供了关键的技术路径。

Abstract: Scientific laboratories increasingly rely on AI systems to reason about experiments, but the physical act of doing science remains largely outside their reach. AI can help read literature, generate hypotheses, and plan protocols, yet the execution of those protocols at the bench still requires a human operator. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide one possible interface between written protocols and robot execution, but existing policies are trained mostly on household and tabletop demonstrations and rarely encounter the instruments, transparent liquids, or fixed protocol workflows found in scientific laboratories. Closing this gap requires both laboratory-specific supervision and a unified learning framework that can accommodate the diverse robot embodiments used to execute experimental protocols. We therefore identify data and embodiment as central bottlenecks alongside model design. To address the data side, we build RoboGenesis, a simulation-based workflow and data engine that composes configured laboratory workflows from atomic skills, validates and filters rollouts, and exports structured demonstrations across supported robot profiles. On the policy side, we present LabVLA, trained with a two-stage recipe: FAST action token pretraining first makes the Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct backbone action aware before any continuous control is learned, and flow matching posttraining then attaches a DiT action expert under knowledge insulation. On the LabUtopia benchmark, LabVLA achieves the highest average success rate among all evaluated baselines under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [51] [An End-to-End Hybrid Framework for Rumour Detection in Low-Resources Algerian Dialect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13411)
*Dihia Lanasri,Fatima Benbarek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对阿尔及利亚社交媒体方言内容的谣言检测混合框架，通过构建专用数据集和转写处理，实现了0.84的F1分数，并证实了领域适配预训练在低资源方言处理中的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体谣言传播迅速，但在阿尔及利亚语境下面临三大挑战：阿尔及利亚方言的高度非正式性与语码转换特性、标注数据极度稀缺，以及现有标准阿拉伯语NLP工具在方言文本上的适用性有限。

Method: 1. 数据构建：整合真实社交媒体内容、FASSILA语料库及合成数据，结合基于相似度的自动标注流程；2. 数据预处理：构建阿语脚本（Arabic script）与Arabizi（拉丁字母拼写的阿拉伯语）之间的转写管道；3. 模型评估：对比了传统机器学习、深度学习、Transformer及混合模型架构。

Result: 混合模型（Transformer嵌入 + 传统分类器）表现最佳，F1分数达到0.84；实验发现领域专用预训练对模型性能的提升效果优于模型参数规模的增加。

Conclusion: 在资源匮乏的阿尔及利亚方言环境下，谣言检测是可行且有效的。研究证明，针对特定领域（社交媒体）进行预训练比单纯追求模型规模更为重要，且结合Transformer嵌入与传统分类器的混合模型在方言谣言检测任务中表现最优。

Abstract: The rapid growth of social media has intensified the spread of rumours. This issue is more challenging in the Algerian context due to the informal and code-switched nature of dialectal content, the scarcity of annotated resources, and the limited effectiveness of standard Arabic NLP tools on dialect text.
  This paper presents an end-to-end rumour detection hybrid framework for Algerian dialect social media content. We build a domain-specific annotated dataset by combining real social media posts, synthetic data, and the FASSILA corpus, with automatic labeling based on a similarity-based annotation process. A transliteration pipeline is also introduced to generate parallel datasets in Arabic script and Arabizi.
  We evaluate multiple approaches, including classical machine learning, deep learning, transformers, and hybrid models. Experimental results show that a hybrid approach combining transformer embeddings with a classical classifier achieves the best performance, reaching an F1-score of 0.84. We also find that domain-specific pre-training is more important than model size, with social media-trained models outperforming larger models trained on formal Arabic corpora.
  These results demonstrate the feasibility of rumour detection in low-resource Algerian dialect settings.

</details>


### [52] [S-GBT: Smooth Growth Bound Tensor for Certified Robustness Against Word Substitution Attacks in NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13439)
*Mohammed Bouri,Mohammed Erradi,Adnane Saoud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为S-GBT的二阶鲁棒性训练方法，通过限制Hessian矩阵来控制模型对词替换攻击的敏感度，在提升鲁棒性的同时保持了良好的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法主要关注一阶灵敏度，忽略了描述梯度剧烈变化的二阶曲率信息，导致模型在梯度变化剧烈时仍易受攻击。

Method: 提出平滑增长界张量（Smooth Growth Bound Tensor, S-GBT），一种对Hessian矩阵进行逐元素界的二阶方法，并将其作为正则化项集成到LSTM和CNN的训练目标中。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，结合一阶和二阶正则化的方法将认证鲁棒准确率提升了高达23.4%，同时保持了具有竞争力的原始准确率。

Conclusion: 通过同时优化一阶梯度敏感性和二阶曲率（Hessian矩阵），可以显著提升NLP模型对词替换攻击的认证鲁棒性，且不会过度牺牲原始准确率。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in Natural Language Processing (NLP), models remain vulnerable to word substitution attacks. Most existing defenses focus on first order sensitivity and measure how much the output changes when the input is slightly perturbed. However, they ignore how this sensitivity evolves, which is described by curvature. When gradients vary sharply, models can still fail. This paper introduces the Smooth Growth Bound Tensor (S-GBT), a second order method that bounds the Hessian element-wise, for which we provide formal theoretical proofs on the resulting robustness bounds. A regularization term is added during training to minimize these bounds. This yields tighter certified robustness against word substitution attacks. The change in the output under word substitution is bounded by both a linear term and a quadratic term. S-GBT is derived for two architectures: Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) and Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN). The method is integrated directly into the training objective. Its effectiveness is evaluated on multiple benchmark datasets. The results show that combining first and second order regularization improves certified robust accuracy by up to 23.4% compared to prior methods, while clean accuracy remains competitive. These findings indicate that controlling both the gradient and its variation is a promising direction for building more robust models.

</details>


### [53] [Ontology Memory-Augmented ASR Correction for Long Text-Speech Interleaved Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13464)
*Xinxin Li,Huiyao Chen,Meishan Zhang,Yunxin Li,Zulong Chen,Zhibo Ren,Xiaoqing Dong Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对长对话中 ASR 纠错依赖局部上下文且易受噪声干扰的问题，提出了一种利用动态本体记忆增强纠错的框架，显著提升了长程对话场景下的纠错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 ASR 纠错方法多侧重于局部上下文，难以在冗余的长对话历史中提取有效的纠错证据，导致长文本-语音交织场景下的纠错效果不佳。

Method: 提出了一种“本体记忆增强”（Ontology Memory-augmented）的 ASR 纠错框架。通过将对话历史组织为可动态更新的本体记忆（存储实体、术语、变体、潜在混淆及语义关系），并将其作为上下文证据进行有针对性的修正。此外，构建了用于长程上下文纠错的数据集 RAMC-Corr。

Result: 在 RAMC-Corr 数据集上的实验结果表明，该框架在 10 种骨干模型组合中的 9 种里均优于直接纠错方法，并能实现更具选择性和基于证据的精准纠错。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的本体记忆增强框架能有效利用长对话上下文，通过结构化存储关键信息来降低冗余和噪声影响，从而提升 ASR 纠错的准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) correction has traditionally focused on isolated utterances or short local contexts. However, as text and speech become increasingly interleaved in long interactions, ASR correction requires conversation-level contextual evidence. Existing ASR correction methods often rely on the current hypothesis or concatenate raw dialogue history. In such contexts, sparse correction evidence can be difficult to locate amid redundancy and noise. Addressing these challenges, we propose an ontology memory-augmented ASR correction framework for long text-speech interleaved conversations. The framework organizes preceding interaction history into a dynamically updatable ontology memory, where entities, terminology, surface variants, potential ASR confusions, and semantic relations are stored as retrievable nodes for context-grounded correction. To evaluate this setting, we construct RAMC-Corr, a dataset derived from MAGIC-RAMC for long-range ASR correction with grounded context. Experiments on RAMC-Corr show that our method improves over direct correction in 9 out of 10 paired backbone-setting combinations and encourages more selective and evidence-grounded corrections for context-dependent ASR errors.

</details>


### [54] [Leveraging Audio-LLMs to Filter Speech-to-Speech Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13507)
*Qixu Chen,Satoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用音频大语言模型（Audio LLM）自动清洗S2ST训练数据的方法，通过Rank-to-Distill策略训练模型直接决策是否保留噪声语音对，有效提升了语音翻译系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模挖掘的语音翻译语料（S2ST）中常包含噪声、对齐错误和语义错误，严重制约模型性能。现有方法往往缺乏高效的自动化过滤手段，因此需要一种能直接处理原始音频、无需人工标注的过滤机制。

Method: 提出了“Rank-to-Distill”两阶段框架：第一阶段利用轻量级排序器从噪声数据中生成伪标签；第二阶段利用生成的伪标签训练一个音频大语言模型（Audio LLM），使其能直接通过原始语音对进行“保留/丢弃”决策。

Result: 在CVSS-C和SpeechMatrix数据集上实验证明，该方法对比未过滤数据有显著提升，其中S2ST的ASR-BLEU指标最高提升了1.4个点。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用音频大语言模型进行端到端语音数据过滤的有效性，能够显著提升S2ST模型的性能，验证了Rank-to-Distill策略在处理大规模噪声语音数据中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large-scale mined corpora provide abundant training data for end-to-end speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) but may contain noise, misalignment, and semantic errors. Filtering noisy data is crucial to maintain robust speech translation performance. We study how to train an audio-language model to make keep/drop decisions on paired speech directly from audio. To obtain reliable supervision without manual labels, we adopt a scalable two-stage Rank-to-Distill strategy. A lightweight ranker generates keep/drop pseudo-labels from noisy speech pairs, then trains an audio large language model to predict keep/drop directly from raw paired speech. The resulting model jointly captures acoustic fidelity and cross-lingual semantic consistency for the selection of speech-conditioned data. Experiments on CVSS-C and SpeechMatrix show consistent improvements over unfiltered training, yielding up to +1.4 ASR-BLEU for end-to-end S2ST.

</details>


### [55] [When Does Mixing Help? Analyzing Query Embedding Interpolation in Multilingual Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13537)
*Tongyao Zhu,Chao-Ming Huang,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现，通过在嵌入层对多语言查询进行插值混合，可以显著提升检索效果；研究揭示了检索模型对语言混合的敏感性受“英语主导地位”和“类型学距离”的系统性影响，且该规律在不同规模的模型中具有稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管混合语言查询在多语言社区中非常普遍，但当前学术界对密集检索模型（dense retrievers）处理此类查询的敏感性缺乏深入的理解。

Method: 提出了一种基于嵌入层插值（embedding-level mixing）的比例控制方法，在 mMARCO 数据集上系统评估 BGE-M3 的检索性能，通过改变平行查询翻译的混合比例来分析检索表现。

Result: 1. 在 105 个案例中的 88 个案例里，通过嵌入插值得到的“最佳混合比例”表现优于纯单语查询；2. 存在显著的“英语主导非对称性”：非英语文档检索受益于混合，而包含英语的文档库则以纯英语查询表现最佳；3. 英语是所有非英语语言检索中最有效的混合伙伴。

Conclusion: 检索模型对语言混合具有结构化且可预测的敏感性。尽管英语存在主导地位，但在非英语文档检索中，混合查询总是优于单一语言，且语言间的类型学距离与混合收益呈负相关。这一结论在不同模型（模型簇）和规模下保持稳健。

Abstract: While mixed-language querying is ubiquitous in multilingual communities, the sensitivity of dense retrievers to such queries remains poorly understood. We present a ratio-controlled study on mMARCO that systematically evaluates retrieval performance by varying the mixing proportion of parallel query translations via embedding-level mixing -- constructing mixed queries as an interpolation of monolingual embeddings. Experiments with BGE-M3 demonstrate that an optimal mixing ratio outperforms the best monolingual endpoint in 88/105 cases. We uncover a distinct asymmetry driven by English dominance: mixing is uniformly beneficial when retrieving from non-English document indices, whereas indices containing English are best served by pure English queries. Furthermore, English acts as the strongest mixing partner for every non-English document language. Finally, when controlling for English dominance, mixing gains correlate negatively with typological distance. We conclude that language-mix sensitivity is structured and predictable, and we validate the robustness of these patterns across model families and scales.

</details>


### [56] [ArogyaSutra: A Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Medical Reasoning in Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13572)
*Tanmoy Kanti Halder,Akash Ghosh,Subhadip Baidya,Arijit Roy,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对医疗MLLM在多语种、低资源环境下推理能力不足的问题，本文提出了医疗多语种数据集ArogyaBodha和基于Actor-Critic的多智能体推理框架ArogyaSutra，有效提升了AI在医疗查询场景中的多语种处理与推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大模型（MLLM）主要以英语为中心，在处理医疗等专门领域，特别是在多语种和低资源地区（如印度农村）的复杂医疗问答与图像分析任务时表现受限，难以实现医疗人工智能的公平普及。

Method: 1. 数据集构建：构建了包含8个异构来源、涵盖31个身体系统、6种成像模态和21个临床领域的大规模多语种医疗QA数据集（ArogyaBodha）。2. 框架设计：提出了ArogyaSutra，采用基于Actor-Critic的多智能体架构，集成工具接地（tool grounding）与双重记忆机制，实现逐步推理，并利用存储的模拟轨迹进行模型蒸馏。

Result: 实验结果表明，该数据集与框架显著提高了所有目标印度语言（Indic languages）的医学推理准确性，消融实验证实了各核心组件对整体性能的贡献。

Conclusion: ArogyaSutra和ArogyaBodha展示了针对医疗领域多语言、多模态任务的有效性，证明了通过专门构建的数据集和Actor-Critic多智能体框架可以显著提升AI在低资源语言环境下的医疗推理能力，为弥合医疗人工智能的不平等鸿沟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promising reasoning capabilities in general domains, yet their performance remains limited in specialized settings such as healthcare, especially in multilingual and low-resource scenarios. This gap is critical in regions like rural India, where patients often express complex medical queries in native Indic languages and rely on multimodal inputs such as medical images. Existing English-centric MLLMs struggle to support such use cases, limiting equitable access to AI-driven healthcare assistance. To address this challenge, we introduce ArogyaBodha, a large-scale multilingual multimodal medical question-answer dataset constructed from eight heterogeneous sources, covering 31 body systems, six imaging modalities, and 21 clinical domains across English and seven major Indian languages. We further propose ArogyaSutra, an actor-critic-based multi-agent framework that integrates tool grounding with dual-memory mechanisms for step-wise, reasoning-aware decision making, and uses stored actor-critic simulation trajectories for distillation. Experiments show that our dataset and framework improve multilingual medical reasoning accuracy across all Indic languages, with ablations validating the contribution of each component. The source code and dataset are available at: https://iitp-cse.github.io/ ArogyaSutra/

</details>


### [57] [One Polluted Page Is Enough: Evaluating Web Content Pollution in Generative Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13610)
*Minghao Luo,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建 FORGE 基准测试发现，搜索增强型 LLM 极易受到网络污染数据的欺骗，进而推荐虚假产品，且现有的推理能力和部分简单防御方案往往无法缓解、甚至加剧这一风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨搜索增强型大型语言模型在处理被污染的网络数据（如虚假评论、误导性推广）时，是否会沦为虚假产品的推广工具。

Method: 引入基准测试框架 FORGE，通过在检索出的网页内容中将真实产品重写为虚假产品来模拟Web内容污染，并在 12 个不同模型上测量模型推荐虚假产品的频率；评估了怀疑提示、模型先验一致性过滤和跨文档证据过滤三种防御措施。

Result: 所有 12 个测试模型均表现出脆弱性。单个页面污染导致被误导率高达 27%，当 Top-3 搜索结果被完全替换时，被误导率高达 73.8%。模型在缺乏相关先验知识的领域更易受影响。推理过程往往会生成虚假的“社会认同”来掩盖错误。现有的防御方案效果有限，有时甚至会适得其反。

Conclusion: 搜索增强型大语言模型（LLMs）对虚假内容非常脆弱，现有的推理能力和部分防御手段甚至可能加剧虚假推荐的生成。研究强调了在检索增强生成（RAG）系统中建立安全性验证机制的必要性。

Abstract: Search-augmented LLMs increasingly mediate everyday consumer recommendations by retrieving live web content. This creates a new risk: generative recommenders may consume polluted web content, such as fake reviews and promotional pages crafted to mislead recommendations. We ask: to what extent do search-augmented LLMs become unwitting promoters of fake products when consuming polluted retrieval results? To answer this, we introduce FORGE (Fake Online Recommendations in Generative Environments), a benchmark for measuring fake-product promotion under controlled web-content pollution. Given an upstream search result, FORGE locally rewrites real products in retrieved web pages into fake ones to simulate web-content pollution, and measures how often the LLM recommends the fake product. FORGE covers 225 real-world products across 15 categories and 5 consumer scenarios. Across 12 commercial and open-weights LLMs, all models are vulnerable: a single polluted page yields fooled rates of up to 27%, while the full top-3 replacement raises this to 73.8%. Vulnerability varies substantially across categories, increasing when models lack stable prior knowledge of the relevant products. Reasoning does not mitigate this vulnerability; instead, it often generates spurious social proof to justify false recommendations. We evaluate three defenses: skepticism prompting and consensus filtering (over model priors or cross-document evidence). Skepticism can exacerbate vulnerability, much like reasoning, while filtering risks suppressing legitimate products. We release FORGE at https://github.com/leoluolol/forge-benchmark.

</details>


### [58] [Beyond Uniform Tokens: Adaptive Compression for Time Series Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13624)
*Jialin Gan,Xin Qiu,Guangzhe Chen,Xue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对时间序列大模型中TS Token与提示词Token处理效率低的问题，提出了一种非对称Token压缩框架，通过频域分析压缩序列，并按层递减提示词Token，显著提升了推理速度与预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列大模型将数值序列与文本提示统一处理，忽略了两者本质上不同的信息结构，导致计算效率低下。论文旨在通过研究Token的非对称性，实现更高效的建模。

Method: 提出了一个自适应Token预算框架：1) 基于频域结构对时间序列（TS）Token进行压缩，利用其频域冗余性进行精简；2) 针对提示词（Prompt）Token，提出了随模型深度逐层递减的动态保留策略。

Result: 在预测、分类、填补和异常检测等任务中，该方法实现了最高7.68倍的推理加速，并在78%的评估设置中提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的非对称Token压缩框架能有效提升时间序列大模型的推理效率与性能，证明了在时间序列建模中针对不同类型Token采取差异化处理策略的必要性与优越性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled time series (TS) analysis by jointly modeling numerical observations and textual context through a shared token interface. However, TS tokens and prompt tokens exhibit fundamentally different information structures, making uniform token processing inefficient. In this paper, we study token efficiency in TS language modeling from an asymmetric-token perspective. We show that TS tokens have highly uneven spectral contributions, where many tokens share redundant frequency patterns while a small subset preserves critical temporal evidence. We also observe that prompt-token influence attenuates with model depth, suggesting that full prompt retention across all layers is unnecessary. Based on these findings, we develop an adaptive token budgeting framework that compresses TS tokens via frequency-domain structure and progressively reduces prompt tokens across layers. Experiments across forecasting, classification, imputation, and anomaly detection demonstrate up to \textit{\textbf{7.68$\times$}} inference acceleration and performance gains in \textit{\textbf{78\%}} of evaluated settings, showing the effectiveness of asymmetric token compression for scalable TS foundation models.

</details>


### [59] [From Tokens to Faces: Investigating Discrete Speech Representations for 3D Facial Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13630)
*Pedro Correa,Olivier Perrotin,Samir Sadok,Paula Costa,Thomas Hueber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了四类语音表示对三维面部动画的影响，指出音素化编码是提升合成质量的关键，并基于此提出了一种统一的 AVTTS 生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音表示的选择对三维面部动画的质量至关重要，但目前尚缺乏对不同表示法在三维人脸合成任务中表现的系统性对比研究。

Method: 比较了四类语音表示方法（SSL特征、神经编解码、ASR风格目标、离散表示）在两种三维面部解码器上的表现；通过客观指标、感知评估以及针对音素和关节变形的探究分析（Probing analysis）进行评估，并提出了一个端到端的 AVTTS（音视频文本转语音）流水线。

Result: 实验表明，语音表示的音素编码能力与面部动画预测的准确性正相关；语义类和标签类表示在动画合成质量上表现出相当的水平。

Conclusion: 在语音驱动的三维面部动画中，对语音进行音素级（phonetic）编码对提升合成质量至关重要。研究证明，使用共享的离散化表示可以实现高质量的语音解码与三维面部动作合成。

Abstract: The choice of speech representation is critical in speech-driven 3D facial animation. Representations differ in what they encode: SSL features emphasize segmental and semantic cues, neural codecs yield latents optimized for acoustic reconstruction, and ASR-style objectives produce label-based spaces. We evaluate four speech representation families for 3D facial synthesis, comparing their facial reconstruction quality across two facial decoders using objective metrics and a perceptual evaluation. We additionally conduct probing analyses that relate tokenized representations to phonetic units and to articulatory deformations. We found that encoding phonetic classes is beneficial for accurate facial animation prediction on both semantic and label-based representations with comparable facial animation quality. From the latter, we introduce an Audio Visual Text-to-Speech (AVTTS) pipeline that leverages, as a shared space, discrete representations to decode speech and 3D facial motion.

</details>


### [60] [Operads for compositional reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13634)
*Nathaniel Bottman,Kyle Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究引入算子论作为 LLM 问题分解的数学基础，通过定义的“问题算子 Q”和“算子一致性”指标，为分析和提高LLM多步推理的准确性提供了理论模型和实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型（LLM）的“问题分解”（question decomposition）策略缺乏严谨的数学基础理论支撑。

Method: 引入算子论（Operad Theory），构建“问题算子 Q”（questions operad Q），将问题模板定义为算子操作，将答案合成定义为算子组合，并将问答模型定义为 Q 上的代数（algebras over Q）；同时提出“算子一致性”度量标准。

Result: 实验表明“算子一致性”与 LLM 的推理准确率呈强正相关，且在四个多跳问答数据集上表现优于标准的温度采样自我一致性（self-consistency）基线。

Conclusion: 提出算子（operads）是建模问题分解的天然数学框架，通过“算子一致性”提供了一种评估并提升LLM多步推理可靠性的新范式。

Abstract: Question decomposition, i.e. breaking a complex query into simpler sub-queries whose answers are composed to produce a final answer, is a widely used strategy for improving LLM reasoning, yet it currently lacks a rigorous mathematical foundation. In this paper, we propose operads, mathematical structures that model many-in, one-out operations and compositions thereof, as a natural framework for describing question decomposition. We define the questions operad $Q$, in which operations correspond to question templates and composition corresponds to substitution of sub-answers, and show how QA models can be interpreted as algebras over $Q$. Beyond reframing existing practice, this operadic perspective points toward new methods, in particular a notion of operadic consistency, which measures whether a QA model's answers agree across the partial collapses of a question decomposition tree. Empirical evaluation of operadic consistency is reported in our companion paper (Bottman, Liu, and Richardson, 2026), which finds it strongly correlated with accuracy across twelve LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets and outperforming standard temperature-based self-consistency baselines. We argue that operads are the natural mathematical home for question decomposition, and that invariants such as operadic consistency open new directions for analyzing and improving the reliability of multi-step reasoning.

</details>


### [61] [Recursive Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13643)
*Elias Lumer,Sahil Sen,Kevin Paul,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了“递归代理框架（RAH）”，通过在代码层级而非模型层级递归派生具备完整工具链的子代理，显著提升了模型在处理4M token级别长上下文推理任务时的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在扩展递归语言模型（RLM）的长上下文推理能力，将递归单元从简单的“模型调用”升级为功能全面的“代理框架（Agent Harness）”，以适应生产环境中复杂、大规模的自动化编码任务。

Method: 提出了“递归代理框架（RAH）”，即由父代理生成并运行可执行脚本，并行派生多个具备完整工具链（文件系统、代码执行、规划）的子代理，而非仅依赖模型调用的简单递归。

Result: 在Oolong-Synthetic数据集上，以GPT-5为骨干模型时，RAH将编码代理性能从71.75%提升至81.36%；使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为骨干模型时，性能进一步提升至89.77%。

Conclusion: RAH（递归代理框架）作为一种代码优先的递归策略，通过解耦推理任务，有效克服了单一模型上下文窗口的限制，显著提升了长上下文推理任务的性能。

Abstract: Recursive language models (RLMs) showed that recursion over model calls is an effective strategy for long-context reasoning, and production coding agents have begun to write code that spawns subagents at scale, most recently in Anthropic's dynamic workflows. We name and study the pattern between these two lines of work, where the recursive unit is a full agent harness with filesystem tools, code execution, and planning rather than a model call with no tools. We call this the Recursive Agent Harness (RAH) and frame it as harness recursion, the code-first extension to the model recursion of RLMs. A parent agent generates and runs an executable script that spawns subagent harnesses in parallel for fine-grained workloads and uses structured function calls for small subtasks. We provide a controlled evaluation on long-context reasoning. With the backbone held fixed at GPT-5 to match the published Codex and RLM baselines, RAH improves the Codex coding-agent baseline from 71.75% to 81.36% on Oolong-Synthetic (199 samples, 13 context-length buckets up to 4M tokens), a gain attributable to the harness rather than the model. With a stronger backbone, Claude Sonnet 4.5, the same design reaches 89.77%.

</details>


### [62] [SkMTEB: Slovak Massive Text Embedding Benchmark and Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13647)
*Marek Šuppa,Andrej Ridzik,Daniel Hládek,Natália Kňažeková,Viktória Ondrejová*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个斯洛伐克语综合文本嵌入基准（SkMTEB），并基于多语言E5模型开发了高效、可本地部署的专用嵌入模型（e5-sk-small/large），填补了低资源语言在该领域的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯洛伐克语作为低资源语言，缺乏综合性的文本嵌入评测基准，且现有专用模型在嵌入任务上表现不佳，难以满足本地部署需求。

Method: 1. 构建了包含31个数据集、7种任务类型的斯洛伐克语综合评测基准SkMTEB；2. 基于多语言E5模型，采用词表裁剪（Vocabulary Trimming）和监督微调（Fine-tuning）策略，训练出e5-sk-small和e5-sk-large模型。

Result: 1. 评测发现指令微调的大型多语言模型表现最佳；2. 开发的e5-sk模型在参数量减少高达62%的情况下，性能仍可媲美专有API，优于现有的斯洛伐克专用NLU模型。

Conclusion: 通过词表裁剪和微调多语言E5模型，成功开发出高性能、高效率且可本地部署的斯洛伐克语专用嵌入模型，并为其他资源匮乏语言提供了统一的评估基准和建模范式。

Abstract: We introduce SkMTEB, the first comprehensive MTEB-style text embedding benchmark for Slovak, a low-resource West Slavic language, comprising 31 datasets across 7 task types -- nearly 4$\times$ the depth of existing multilingual benchmark coverage for Slovak. Our evaluation of 31 embedding models reveals that large instruction-tuned multilingual models achieve the strongest performance, while existing Slovak-specific models trained for NLU tasks transfer poorly to embedding tasks. To address the need for efficient, locally-deployable Slovak embeddings, we develop \texttt{e5-sk-small} (45M parameters) and \texttt{e5-sk-large} (365M) by applying vocabulary trimming and fine-tuning to Multilingual E5 models. Despite size reductions of up to 62\%, our open-source models achieve competitive performance with proprietary APIs while remaining locally deployable for semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We release the benchmark, models, datasets, and code openly, hoping our approach offers a replicable path for other under-resourced languages.

</details>


### [63] [Operadic consistency: a label-free signal for compositional reasoning failures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13649)
*Nathaniel Bottman,Yinhong Liu,Kyle Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“操作一致性（OC）”的诊断评估指标。该指标通过验证模型对复杂查询的直接回答与对其分解部分的组合回答是否一致，有效检测 LLM 推理失败。实验证明，OC 在多跳 QA 任务中展现出比传统方法更强、更稳健的置信度预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在推理阶段检测 LLM 推理失败的现有方法（如自一致性、P(True) 等）在处理复杂多跳问题时存在局限性，稳定性不足，因此需要一种更稳健、无需标注的置信度评估信号。

Method: 受到操作子理论（Operad theory）启发，提出“操作一致性”（Operadic Consistency, OC）方法。该方法要求模型直接回答复杂查询的结果，应与其将该查询分解并重新组合后得出的结果保持一致。研究在 12 个不同规模的 LLM 上，通过四个多跳 QA 数据集验证了该信号。

Result: OC 在所有测试数据集上与准确率的相关性极高（Pearson r 在 0.86 到 0.94 之间），表现出比 CoT-SC 更强的鲁棒性。在选择性预测任务中，OC 相比 CoT-SC 在同等预算下实现了明显的 AUARC 和 AUROC 提升。即使在从模型自身的思维链（CoT）中提取分解结果时，OC 也展现出了优越的置信度评估能力。

Conclusion: “操作一致性”（OC）是一种衡量 LLM 推理可靠性的稳健且强有力的新信号。它优于传统的自一致性（CoT-SC）和语义熵，特别是在处理多跳复杂问题时，表现出极高且统一的相关性，并显著提升了模型的选择性预测性能。

Abstract: Detecting LLM reasoning failures at inference time without ground-truth labels has motivated a wide range of confidence baselines, including self-consistency, semantic entropy, and P(True), built on within-question sampling and self-evaluation. Operad theory, the formalism for systems built by iterated substitution, suggests a complementary diagnostic: a model's direct answer to a compositional query should agree with the answer it produces by composing a stated decomposition of the same query. We instantiate this idea as operadic consistency (OC), a per-question signal. Across twelve instruction-tuned LLMs (4B to 671B parameters, open-weights and closed-source) on four multi-hop QA datasets, OC is strongly correlated with accuracy on every dataset (Pearson $r \in [0.86, 0.94]$, all $p \leq 0.0004$), and is the only signal we evaluate with $r \geq 0.85$ uniformly across all four datasets. Chain-of-thought self-consistency (CoT-SC; Wang et al., 2023) matches OC on HotpotQA and DROP ($r = 0.93, 0.87$) but drops to $r \approx 0.45$ on MuSiQue and StrategyQA. At the per-question level, OC contributes information beyond CoT-SC and semantic entropy on every dataset (cluster-robust $p \leq 10^{-16}$ for the OC coefficient), and the conclusion is robust to additionally controlling for constructed decomposition-aware baselines ($p \leq 10^{-13}$). The same signal yields selective-prediction improvements (accuracy at fixed coverage) over a tuned CoT-SC baseline at the equal-cost $K = 3$ budget (AUARC lifts of +0.086 to +0.096 and AUROC lifts of +0.092 to +0.164; 95% CIs exclude zero on every cell). On five frontier thinking models, where the decomposition is extracted from the model's own chain of thought, the same equal-cost comparison gives positive selective-prediction point-estimate lift on all 16 (dataset, budget, metric) cells tested, with 95% CIs excluding zero on 12 of the 16.

</details>


### [64] [HyperTool: Beyond Step-Wise Tool Calls for Tool-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13663)
*Yaxin Du,Yifan Zhou,Yujie Ge,Jiajun Wang,Xianghe Pang,Shuo Tang,Tuney Zheng,Bryan Dai,Jian Yang,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyperTool 通过将繁琐的多步原子工具调用“打包”为可执行的代码块，优化了 LLM 的推理轨迹，在保障确定性逻辑的同时，显著提升了工具使用任务的执行效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 智能体过度依赖原子化的工具调用，导致模型上下文窗口消耗过大，且需处理复杂的低级数据流，存在“执行粒度不匹配”问题。

Method: 提出 HyperTool 接口，允许模型通过代码块调用多个工具并内部管理数据传递，实现执行粒度的“折叠”；通过合成训练轨迹并结合 MCP（Model Context Protocol）环境进行训练与验证。

Result: 在 MCP-Universe 基准测试中，Qwen3-32B 和 Qwen3-8B 的准确率分别提升至 35.29% 和 33.33%，全面超越 GPT-OSS 和 Kimi-k2.5 等现有模型。

Conclusion: HyperTool 通过引入更高层次的执行抽象（将确定性工作流合并为单一调用），有效解决了 LLM 在处理复杂工具链时的性能瓶颈，显著提升了多步工具调用的任务准确率。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents commonly rely on step-wise atomic tool calls, where each invocation, observation, and value transfer is exposed in the main reasoning trace. This creates an \emph{execution-granularity mismatch}: locally deterministic tool workflows are unfolded into repeated model-visible decisions, consuming context and forcing the model to manage low-level dataflow in the trace. We introduce \textbf{HyperTool}, a unified executable MCP-style tool interface that changes the model-visible unit of tool execution. A model invokes HyperTool with a code block that can call existing tools through their original schemas, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, folding deterministic tool subroutines into a single outer call. To train models to use this interface, we synthesize HyperTool-format trajectories from cross-tool compositional tasks and verify them in real MCP environments. On MCP-Universe, HyperTool improves average accuracy from 15.69\% to 35.29\% on Qwen3-32B and from 9.93\% to 33.33\% on Qwen3-8B, and surpass GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5 on average accuracy, showing that our HyperTool can substantially improve multi-step tool use.

</details>


### [65] [Influcoder: Distilling Decoders' Gradient Influence Rankings into an Encoder for Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13668)
*Dimitri Kachler,Damien Sileo,Pascal Denis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为了解决传统数据归因方法在大规模数据集上计算缓慢且内存消耗大的问题，本文提出了 Influcoder，这是一种高效且具有成本效益的基于影响力的分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于影响函数（Influence Functions）的数据归因方法虽然在评估训练数据对模型输出的影响方面有效，但在大规模数据集上存在计算速度慢、存储成本高的瓶颈，难以实际应用。

Method: Influcoder（具体技术细节未在摘要中详细展开，但定性为一种优化的高效影响函数计算方法）。

Result: 提出了一种名为 Influcoder 的方法，相比传统方法，它能以更快的速度和更低的成本实现大规模数据归因。

Conclusion: Influcoder 是一种在大规模数据集上进行高效且经济的基于影响函数的数据归因（Data Attribution）解决方案，解决了现有方法在速度和存储方面的局限性。

Abstract: With the growth of LLMs' (Large Language Models) capabilities, there has been an increasing push to curate high quality datasets by filtering samples in the training data. In general, Data Attribution (DA) methods aim to estimate how individual samples in a training dataset can precondition a model to generate certain outputs. As an example, one might be interested in which samples in the data could be the source of toxic behavior after training the LLM. Many methods quantify this conditioning through the paradigm of influence functions. While methods of this family are effective in its function, they lack the necessary processing speed and storage compactness to be practically implemented on large datasets. We propose a method, Influcoder, as a quick and cost-effective approach to influence-based Data Attribution at scale.

</details>


### [66] [Learning to Reason by Analogy via Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13680)
*Zilin Xiao,Qi Ma,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Xintao Chen,Avinash Atreya,Hanjie Chen,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对复杂推理任务，提出一种名为RA-RFT的新框架，通过“推理感知”的检索机制取代传统的语义检索，教会模型进行类比推理，从而显著提升模型的数学解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于词汇或语义相似度的RAG方法无法应对复杂推理任务的需求，因为语义相似的问题可能需要不同的解决策略，而表面不同但本质相似的问题却需要相同的推理逻辑。

Method: 提出了“检索增强强化微调”（RA-RFT）框架，包含两个核心步骤：1. 使用“黄金相关性蒸馏”训练检索器，按推理效益而非语义重叠度对上下文进行排序；2. 利用检索到的类比演示数据对策略模型进行强化学习微调（利用可验证的回报信号）。

Result: RA-RFT在数学推理基准测试中优于标准的强化学习微调方法。在AIME 2025测试中，Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B模型的average@32准确率分别提升了7.1和2.8个百分点。

Conclusion: “推理感知”检索是一种独立且互补的优化维度，通过学习类比推理而非单纯依赖语义相似度，能显著提升大模型在复杂数学推理任务中的表现。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has become a standard mechanism for grounding language models in external knowledge, yet conventional retrieval based on lexical or semantic similarity is poorly suited for complex reasoning tasks: a semantically similar problem may demand an entirely different solution strategy, while a superficially different problem may share the same underlying reasoning pattern. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RA-RFT), a post-training framework that teaches language models to reason by analogy. RA-RFT uses gold-relevance distillation to train a retriever that ranks contexts by expected reasoning benefit rather than semantic overlap, and then fine-tunes the policy model via reinforcement fine-tuning methods with retrieved analogous demonstrations, so the model learns to leverage reasoning traces under verifiable outcome rewards. We further analyze the diversity of retrieved contexts and find that reasoning-aware retrieval surfaces complementary solution strategies that provide distinct reasoning scaffolds for individual problems. Across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, RA-RFT consistently outperforms standard reinforcement fine-tuning methods. For example, it improves AIME 2025 average@32 accuracy by 7.1 and 2.8 points over GRPO for Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B respectively -- suggesting that reasoning-aware retrieval is a complementary axis of improvement and orthogonal to advances in reward design or training curricula.

</details>


### [67] [EvoArena: Tracking Memory Evolution for Robust LLM Agents in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13681)
*Jundong Xu,Qingchuan Li,Jiaying Wu,Yihuai Lan,Shuyue Stella Li,Huichi Zhou,Bowen Jiang,Lei Wang,Jun Wang,Anh Tuan Luu,Caiming Xiong,Hae Won Park,Bryan Hooi,Zhiyuan Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM代理在动态环境下的适应性挑战，本文提出了EvoArena基准测试和EvoMem记忆演进机制，实验证明该方法能有效提升智能体处理环境变化及连续任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理评估大多基于静态环境，而真实世界的部署环境是动态变化的，智能体需要具备持续对齐知识、技能和行为以适应环境演变的能力。

Method: 1. 提出EvoArena基准测试：通过终端、软件和社交领域的一系列渐进式更新来模拟环境变化；2. 提出EvoMem记忆模式：一种基于补丁（patch-based）的记忆架构，将记忆演变记录为结构化的更新历史，使智能体能够通过记忆的变化来推理环境的演进。

Result: 1. 智能体在EvoArena上的平均准确率仅为39.6%；2. EvoMem在EvoArena上平均提升了1.5%的性能，并在GAIA和LoCoMo等标准基准上分别提升了6.1%和4.8%；3. 在需要完成连续演进子任务的链式场景中，EvoMem提升了3.7%的准确率；4. 机制分析表明EvoMem增强了对完整演进环境状态的捕捉能力。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型代理在动态环境中表现不足，模型评估与记忆体系需要从静态范式转向演进式（evolutionary）范式，以确保在真实世界部署中的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have achieved strong performance on a wide range of benchmarks, yet most evaluations assume static environments. In contrast, real-world deployment is inherently dynamic, requiring agents to continually align their knowledge, skills, and behavior with changing environments and updated task conditions. To address this gap, we introduce EvoArena, a benchmark suite that models environment changes as sequences of progressive updates across terminal, software, and social domains. We further propose EvoMem, a patch-based memory paradigm that records memory evolution as structured update histories, enabling agents to reason about environmental evolution through changes in their memory. Experiments show that current agents struggle on EvoArena, achieving an average accuracy of 39.6% across evolving terminal, software, and social-preference domains. EvoMem consistently improves performance, yielding an average gain of 1.5% on EvoArena and also improving standard benchmarks such as GAIA and LoCoMo by 6.1% and 4.8%. Beyond individual tasks, EvoMem further improves chain-level accuracy by 3.7% on EvoArena, where success requires completing a consecutive sequence of related evolutionary subtasks. Mechanistic analysis shows that EvoMem improves evidence capture in the memory, indicating better preservation of complete evolving environment states. Our results highlight the importance of modeling evolution in both evaluation and memory for reliable agent deployment.

</details>

### [68] [Identifiability Without Gaussianity: Symbolic World Models and Near-Infinite Temporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12471)
*Seth Dobrin,Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了物理扎根符号架构（PGSA），解决了现有基于统计的世界模型在处理非高斯物理过程时无法保持长期时间一致性的根本限制，并利用Lean 4形式化验证了其在理论上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决Klindt等人提出的JEPAs在非高斯物理系统中存在representation error随时间单调递增的问题，即打破统计世界模型在处理非高斯系统时的本质性能极限。

Method: 1. 分析并批判了基于统计对齐机制的JEPAs在非高斯系统下的局限性；2. 提出了物理扎根符号架构（PGSA）；3. 使用Lean 4定理证明器对核心定理进行了形式化验证。

Result: 1. 证明了PGSA能够在任意物理状态分布下实现精确的线性可识别性；2. 证明了PGSA的单步误差仅受限于数值精度，实现了近乎无限的时间一致性；3. 证明了统计世界模型在非高斯系统中无论如何增加模型容量或数据量都无法达到上述特性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了对于非高斯物理系统，统计世界模型存在本质的误差发散限制。相比之下，符号扎根（Symbolic grounding）是实现近乎无限时间一致性（near-infinite temporal consistency）的充要条件，证明了在因果物理规律中引入符号化处理（PGSA）优于基于统计对齐的架构。

Abstract: Klindt, LeCun, and Balestriero (arXiv:2605.26379) proved that Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) achieve linear identifiability, the linear recovery of the world's true latent variables, if and only if the world's latent dynamics follow a Gaussian, stationary process. This Gaussian boundary implies a fundamental limit on temporal consistency: for any non-Gaussian physical system, the representation error of a statistical World Model grows monotonically with time. We prove that this limit is an artifact of the statistical alignment mechanism, not a property of World Models in general. We introduce the Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA) and prove three results: (1) a PGSA achieves exact linear identifiability for all physical regimes, regardless of the latent distribution; (2) the per-step error of a PGSA is bounded by numerical precision alone; and (3) as a direct consequence, a PGSA maintains temporal consistency for an unbounded number of transitions, a property we term near-infinite temporal consistency. We further prove that statistical World Models cannot achieve this property for any non-Gaussian system, regardless of model capacity or the volume of training data. The algebraic cores of four of the theorems are formalized in Lean 4 with Mathlib4 v4.31.0 (zero sorry placeholders); the Klindt et al. converse is taken as an external premise. The contrast establishes that symbolic grounding in the causal generator of the world's dynamics is the sufficient condition and, in non-Gaussian regimes, the only condition for near-infinite temporal consistency.

</details>


### [69] [Epistemic Uncertainty Is Not the Reducible Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12646)
*Robin Young*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文指出传统认知不确定性的定义与度量存在逻辑冲突，提出三分类架构以取代二元论，并证明了常用的集成模型不确定性指标本质上反映的是训练细节而非真正的模型认知不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测不确定性文献中，将“认知不确定性”定义为“可通过增加数据消除的不确定性”，并将其与“互信息”这一度量方式等同。作者旨在解决这一概念定义与实用度量之间的不一致性矛盾。

Method: 通过理论推导证明了传统定义（随数据增加而减少）与度量（互信息）之间的不一致性；提出了基于（不确定性，获取类）的分析框架；利用有限样本证伪测试和种子遍历（seed-swept）实验对结论进行了验证。

Result: 证明了传统互信息定义无法完全捕捉可消除性；指出同分布数据无法减少模型机制产生的不可约不确定性；揭示了集成模型的分歧（ensemble disagreement）本质上是训练动态和超参数噪声的反映，而非真正的认知不确定性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了现有预测不确定性框架存在逻辑谬误。作者提出了包含“偶然不确定性”、“样本可约认知不确定性”和“机制不可约认知不确定性”的三分法，并证明了传统的集成不确定性估计往往反映的是训练过程特征而非真实的认知不确定性。

Abstract: The standard taxonomy of predictive uncertainty defines epistemic uncertainty as the part removable by collecting more data, while the standard measure identifies it with a mutual-information term. We prove the definition and the measure are extensionally inconsistent. On an explicit construction, the measure assigns all uncertainty to the epistemic class, yet no quantity of training data reduces it. Reducibility is instead a property of the pair (uncertainty, acquisition class), and the dichotomy resolves into three parts: aleatoric, sample-reducible epistemic, and mechanism-reducible epistemic uncertainty. An exact identity for the value of an observation shows that in-distribution data never reduces mechanism-irreducible uncertainty and generically increases it. Ensemble disagreement, the deployed epistemic estimate, tracks the training procedure rather than the epistemic term. It collapses to zero beneath a positive truth under consistent training, and equals hyperparameter-scaled initialization noise under interpolation. A finite-sample falsification test and seed-swept experiments confirm the theory.

</details>


### [70] [Prediction-Powered Causal Inference by Automatic Debiased Machine Learning and Semi-Supervised Riesz Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12892)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半监督因果推理框架（DML-PPCI），通过整合无标签辅助数据和去偏机器学习方法，有效降低了因果及结构参数的估计方差，并提供了理论效率界证明及相应的估计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在大量无标签辅助变量的半监督场景下，现有的因果推理方法未充分利用这些资源，导致参数估计效率受限。研究旨在构建能利用辅助信息以降低参数估计渐进方差的新框架。

Method: 1. 提出了“预测驱动因果推理”（PPCI）框架；2. 推导了存在辅助回归变量时的有效影响函数（EIF）和效率界；3. 将EIF与去偏机器学习（DML）结合，构建了EE-DML-PPCI和TMLE-DML-PPCI两种估计器；4. 开发了具有收敛性保证的半监督广义Riesz回归方法，用于估计Riesz表示器。

Result: 研究表明，利用辅助回归变量可以实现比仅使用标签数据更小的渐进方差；提出的DML-PPCI估计器满足所推导的理论效率界，并在理论上保证了估计的收敛性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的DML-PPCI框架实现了在半监督场景下对因果和结构参数的高效估计，证明了利用无标签辅助数据的半监督方法能够突破仅依靠标签数据所带来的效率边界。

Abstract: This study investigates semiparametric efficient estimation of causal and structural parameters in a semi-supervised setting. In our setting, unlabeled auxiliary regressors are available in addition to labeled observations consisting of outcomes and regressors. Our goal is to construct estimators of causal and structural parameters whose asymptotic variances are smaller than those of estimators constructed using only labeled data. We refer to this framework as prediction-powered causal inference (PPCI). We first derive the efficient influence function and the efficiency bound, which imply that the use of auxiliary regressors can attain a smaller asymptotic variance than the efficiency bound attainable from labeled observations alone. Then, by combining the efficient influence function with the debiased machine learning (DML) framework, we propose methods that we call DML-PPCI. If we construct an estimating-equation estimator, we refer to the method as EE-DML-PPCI; if we construct a targeted-learning estimator, we refer to the method as TMLE-DML-PPCI. The asymptotic variances of both estimators match our derived efficiency bound. In the construction of the estimators, estimation of the efficient influence function plays an important role. In our study, the efficient influence function is also a Neyman orthogonal score, which depends on the Riesz representer and the regression function. For Riesz representer estimation, we develop semi-supervised generalized Riesz regression with convergence rate guarantees.

</details>


### [71] [Robust State-Conditional Feature-Weighted Jump Models for Temporal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13146)
*Federico P. Cortese,Alessio Farcomeni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种稳健的特征加权时间跳跃聚类模型，通过引入Tukey双平方损失和状态依赖特征权重，显著提升了在异常值存在时的聚类准确性和特征识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对时间依赖性聚类中，现有的模型往往难以处理异常值，且缺乏在不同状态下识别特定特征重要性的灵活性，旨在提升模型在波动环境下的聚类稳健性和特征解释力。

Method: 提出了一种带特征权重的稳健跳跃模型（robust feature-weighted jump model），采用Tukey的双平方损失函数（Tukey's biweight loss function）以增强对异常值的稳健性，引入惩罚项促进时间上的平滑过渡，并增加参数以控制特征权重在不同状态下的变异性。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法在恢复聚类序列和特征选择方面的性能优于对比模型，尤其是在存在异常值的情况下效果显著。

Conclusion: 该模型能够准确恢复真实的聚类序列并识别关键特征，在处理含有异常值的数据时表现优于现有方法。通过Kosovo冲突杀人案分析和欧洲宏观经济数据应用，验证了该模型在现实世界复杂数据集中的有效性和稳健性。

Abstract: We propose a robust feature-weighted jump model for time-dependent clustering. A penalty is used to encourage smoothness of transitions over time, while robustness is achieved through the use of a Tukey's biweight loss function. An additional parameter controls the variability of feature weights across states, allowing the model to assign state-specific relevance to each feature. We illustrate in simulation how the method accurately recovers the true cluster sequence and reliably identifies relevant features, outperforming competing approaches, particularly in the presence of outliers. We conclude with two empirical applications, one on the number of conflict-related homicides in Kosovo in the period 1998-2000, and another on macroeconomic performance of twelve European countries in the period 1949-2024.

</details>


### [72] [ProtoX-AD: Self-Explainable Time Series Anomaly Detection and Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13277)
*Aitor Sánchez-Ferrera,Elisabeth Wetzer,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Michael Kampffmeyer,Robert Jenssen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ProtoX-AD 提出了一种基于原型的新型自监督时间序列异常检测框架，旨在在保持高性能的前提下，为检测到的异常提供更明确的语义解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自监督时间序列异常检测（TSAD）方法多为黑箱模型，虽然检测性能优秀，但缺乏可解释性，难以解析检测到的异常特征。

Method: 提出了一种原型驱动的自监督框架（ProtoX-AD），通过学习“变换感知”的潜在表示与可解释原型相结合，支持异常检测与异常配置文件的识别。此外，分析了变换设计对检测性能和可解释性的影响。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法不仅实现了准确的异常检测，还通过原型提供了比现有基线更可靠且具有语义意义的异常解释。

Conclusion: ProtoX-AD 在维持与黑箱模型相当的检测性能的同时，提供了更具一致性和语义可解释性的异常分析功能，验证了原型学习在可解释时间序列异常检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in time series anomaly detection (TSAD) have highlighted the effectiveness of self-supervised classification-based approaches. These methods apply transformations to normal training samples, training a classifier to recognize transformation-specific patterns that help identify anomalies through increased classification errors. Despite their strong performance, a significant challenge is their lack of explainability, as they provide limited insight into the characteristics of flagged anomalies. To address this limitation, we propose ProtoX-AD, a prototype-based self-explainable framework for self-supervised TSAD. ProtoX-AD learns transformation-aware latent representations alongside interpretable prototypes, enabling both accurate anomaly detection and the identification of distinct anomalous profiles through prototype-based explanations. Additionally, it allows for systematic analysis of how transformation design impacts detection performance and explainability. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that ProtoX-AD achieves detection performance comparable to its black-box counterparts while offering more consistent and semantically meaningful explanations than existing explainable baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aitorzan3/ProtoX-AD.

</details>


### [73] [Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13295)
*Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification Cristian Buoncompagni,Stefano Pellegrino,Giulia Vannucci,Roberta Siciliano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“同步潜在预算树”（SLBT）的新型决策树框架，它能有效整合分层变量作为控制因素，通过基于混合模型的裂分逻辑，在维持模型可解释性的前提下，提升了对复杂数据结构的分类与分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可解释人工智能（XAI）背景下，虽然单树结构易于解释，但传统的决策树增长过程无法有效处理作为控制变量或混杂因素的分层变量，因此需要一种能同时处理此类因素的概率机器学习框架。

Method: 提出了一种基于模型的分裂规则，将子节点解释为“同步混合模型”（如SLBT）的潜在组件；利用神经网络的研究视角，通过最小二乘法进行参数估计；配套开发了可视化交互工具及针对类别不平衡的处理措施。

Result: 成功构建了SLBT框架，通过鲁棒的参数估计和可视化手段，为临床研究（如肌萎缩侧索硬化症中性别差异的疾病进展分析）提供了解释性强的建模方案，并开源了相关代码库。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能够处理分层因素（如时间、空间或人口统计变量）的“同步潜在预算树”（SLBT）框架，在保持决策树高度可解释性的同时，增强了其对潜在混杂因素的建模能力。

Abstract: In the era of Explainable Artificial Intelligence, there is a renewed focus on single trees for their ease of interpretation. This paper introduces Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees, a probabilistic machine learning framework for classification trees in the presence of a stratification factor such as a temporal, spatial, or demographic variable, acting as a control variable or potential confounder. Standard tree growth procedures are not designed to optimize a conditional split rule. A model-based split rule is proposed in which child nodes are interpreted as latent components of a simultaneous mixture model, such as the Simultaneous Latent Budget Model and its constrained versions, fitted to the parent node. Mixing parameters drive the observations, differently for each group, to the child nodes whereas latent budgets parameters update the response classes profile of each level of the control variable. Parameters are estimated by least squares considering a neural network perspective of the model. An informative tree structure can be interactively visualized with interpretation aids on the node and the paths, including visual pruning and decision tree selection procedure. Suitable measures are proposed to handle an unbalanced response class distribution. The proposed methodology is applied to investigate gender-related differences in disease progression of Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis. The SLBT library with the various tree-based algorithms is available in the linked GitHub repository.

</details>


### [74] [Majority-of-Three is Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13614)
*Divit Rawal,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了在可实现PAC学习中，使用三个独立一致性分类器进行多数投票即为最优学习方案，从而简化了该领域的算法结构与分析复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在寻找一种更加简洁、直观的证明方式，以确定在可实现PAC学习设置中，何种投票策略能达到最优学习效果，并降低现有相关分析的复杂度。

Method: 通过对三个独立一致性分类器的多数投票逻辑进行数学证明，简化了之前关于投票学习器（如S. Hanneke的算法、K. Green Larsen对Bagging分析）的算法架构及概率分析方法。

Result: 证明了多数投票法在指定设置下的最优性，并成功简化了早期复杂的研究方案。

Conclusion: 在可实现（realizable）PAC学习框架下，三个独立一致性分类器的多数投票法（majority vote）是一种最优学习器。该结论不仅证明了最简单投票方案的最优性，还简化了算法结构及相关概率分析。

Abstract: We give a short proof that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers is an optimal learner in the realizable PAC setting. This proves optimality for the simplest voting scheme, while simplifying both the algorithmic structure and the probabilistic analysis of previous voting learners, including the algorithm of S. Hanneke and the analysis of bagging by K. Green Larsen.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

### [75] [Learning to Assist: Collaborative VLAs for Implicit Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12475)
*Leo Xu,Letian Li,Alex Cuellar,Michael Hagenow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在人机协作中的应用，指出其动作分块策略易产生过早协助问题，并提出了一种推理时引导方法来优化模型表现，经用户研究证实该方法有效提升了长序列任务的协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的许多人机协作系统依赖人工设计的流水线，缺乏灵活性和可扩展性；同时也为了探讨端到端训练的VLA模型在实际协作环境中的表现及其局限性。

Method: 1. 对现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在人机协作场景下进行评估；2. 识别出动作分块（action-chunking）策略在隐含人机协作（implicit HRC）中导致任务转换阶段出现动作泄露的失败模式；3. 提出了一种推理时引导方法（Inference-time steering）以修正过早的协助行为；4. 进行16名参与者的用户研究，验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 实验表明，模型在长执行跨度下会出现动作泄露，导致机器人表现出过早协助等失败模式；所提出的引导方法成功减少了此类错误，且在用户研究中，使用该方法的策略相比短跨度策略显著提高了协作速度并降低了失败率。

Conclusion: 通过在推理阶段引入引导方法（Inference-time steering），可以有效缓解端到端VLA模型在人机协作中出现的过早协助（premature assistance）问题，从而支持更长的执行跨度，提升协作效率和任务完成率。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) combines the complementary strengths of humans and robots to improve task efficiency. However, many existing collaborative systems rely on hand-engineered pipelines, limiting their scalability and flexibility for new tasks. In this work, we show that models trained end-to-end with imitation learning, specifically vision-language-action (VLA) models, can support collaborative manipulation, and characterize the key factors affecting their real-world performance. We evaluate two state-of-the-art models and identify a failure mode of action-chunking policies in implicit HRC, where demonstration action leakage (i.e., action chunks crossing latent task transitions) can cause premature assistive behavior. We find that this issue increases with longer execution horizons and occurs in real-world collaborative VLA systems, such as when a robot attempts to hand over a tool before the person is ready. We propose an inference-time steering method to mitigate these erroneous assistive actions while preserving policy performance. Finally, through a 16-participant user study on a long-horizon collaborative assembly task, we show that steering enables a longer execution horizon while mitigating premature assistance, leading to faster collaboration and fewer failures compared to a shorter-horizon policy.

</details>


### [76] [Action-Effect Memory Pretraining for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12499)
*Yijing Zhou,Qiwei Liang,Sitong Zhuang,Jiaxi Li,Xianpeng Wang,Boyang Cai,Yunyang Mo,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEM 是一种结合视觉-动作历史的机器人预训练框架，通过 Mamba 编码时序上下文，有效解决了机器人操作中的部分可观测性问题，提升了策略执行的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人预训练多聚焦于单帧视觉特征，忽略了机器人操作中固有的时序依赖性和部分可观测性问题，导致在复杂任务中表征能力不足。

Method: 提出 AEM（Action-Effect Memory）预训练框架：通过交替视觉和动作特征，利用掩码建模（Masked Modeling）学习动作条件下的状态演化，并引入 Mamba 架构作为高效的单向量时序瓶颈及全局上下文生成器。

Result: AEM 在模拟和真实物理环境中均显著提升了 Diffusion Policy 和 Flow Policy 的性能，在杂乱场景及非马尔可夫任务中表现优于现有的单帧预训练和帧堆叠方法，且降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: AEM 框架证明了将动作-视觉历史整合进单一时序瓶颈的有效性，为机器人操作提供了比单帧编码更优越、更高效的预训练范式。

Abstract: We present AEM, an Action-Effect Memory pretraining framework for robot manipulation that learns compact temporal representations from vision-action history. Unlike prior robot representation pretraining methods that mainly focus on single-frame visual encoding, AEM targets the temporal nature of manipulation, where the current observation alone is often insufficient under partial observability. AEM models manipulation as an action-driven interaction process by interleaving visual and action features and applying masked modeling to recover missing content from incomplete histories, thereby learning action-conditioned state evolution. The Mamba-encoded output of the final vision token is used as a compact history representation, serving as the global context for decoding and downstream control. This design preserves a single-vector temporal bottleneck while keeping inference efficient. We evaluate AEM with Diffusion Policy and Flow Policy. AEM consistently improves manipulation performance in both simulation and real-world settings, outperforming baselines across clean scenes, cluttered and random scenes, and non-Markovian tasks. Ablation studies further show that history-aware pretraining surpasses single-frame pretraining and direct frame stacking, while reducing inference latency and computational cost.

</details>


### [77] [Foresight: Iterative Reasoning About Clues that Matter for Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12550)
*Arthur Zhang,Carl Qi,Donne Su,Xiangyun Meng,Amy Zhang,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Foresight 是一个基于预训练 VLM 的测试时导航框架，通过引入“提案-评价-优化”的迭代循环及人类反馈强化学习，有效提升了机器人处理开放世界缺失地图场景的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地图缺失导航方法依赖于已知的导航因子或封闭集分类，难以处理开放世界中模糊的目标指令和未知的环境线索；研究人员旨在利用预训练 VLM 的泛化能力，使其能够自适应地识别相关线索并指导动态运动规划。

Method: 提出 Foresight 框架：1. 使用微调后的 VLM 在测试时交替进行“生成运动规划”与“基于目标和环境的评价”；2. 通过前序评价引导后续规划，实现迭代优化；3. 采用基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）训练奖励模型，以对齐开放集行为偏好。

Result: 在离线与6个真实世界场景的测试中，Foresight 使平均任务成功率提升了 37%，任务干预次数减少了 52%，且能在 Jetson AGX Orin 边缘计算设备上实现实时运行。

Conclusion: Foresight 证明了利用预训练 VLM 通过测试时（test-time）方案进行迭代运动规划与优化，能有效解决开放世界中地标缺失导航的问题。通过人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）微调后的 VLM，在无需已知导航因子的情况下，实现了更稳健的导航表现。

Abstract: Open-world mapless navigation from sparse language instructions requires resolving underspecified goals and inferring which environmental cues are relevant for reaching the goal. For instance, reaching an out-of-view destination may require interpreting ramps, signs, or detours that reveal where to go or which route to take. Prior works are limited by their reliance on known navigation factors and closed-set factor categories, or identify cues before motion planning and miss plan-dependent cues. We argue that pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can discover novel instruction-relevant cues, but require adaptation to focus on which cues matter and how they should influence motion planning. We realize these ideas in Foresight, a test-time framework in which a finetuned VLM alternates between proposing image-space motion plans and critiquing them using the language goal and visual context. Subsequent plans are conditioned on prior critiques, enabling iterative motion refinement before execution. To align plan critiques and refinements with open-set behavior preferences, we learn a reward model from human feedback and use it to post-train the VLM with reinforcement learning in the plan-critique loop. In offline evaluations and 6 real-world environments, Foresight improves average task success by 37% and reduces interventions per mission by 52% relative to state-of-the-art test-time reasoning and foundation-model baselines, while running in real-time on a Jetson AGX Orin.
