Extensive experiments demonstrate that IterCAD achieves highly competitive performance across multiple benchmarks, significantly outperforming existing approaches in both code executability and geometric precision, while exhibiting superior capabilities in closed-loop iterative refinement.

</details>


### [183] [A Quantitative Experimental Repeated Measures Study of Training Dynamics in a Small Llama Style Language Model Under a Compute-Aware Token Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13370)
*Joe Dwyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对小型Llama模型受限训练过程的定量分析发现，训练过程中存在明显的性能回退和不稳定性，强调了评估模型时关注全程训练轨迹而非仅看终点指标的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的评估往往只关注单一的终点性能，忽视了模型在受限计算资源下训练过程中的复杂动态行为，如不稳定性、性能倒退和收益递减等问题。

Method: 采用重复测量实验设计，对426万参数的小型Llama风格模型，在TinyStories语料库下进行6次独立训练（共21个训练区间，126个观测点），记录验证损失、验证困惑度、滚动波动率、回退行为及尖峰行为，并进行重复测量方差分析（ANOVA）。

Result: 实验显示验证指标呈先快速改善后非单调恶化的趋势；模型在约400万Token处表现最佳（验证损失2.7996），随后在2000万Token前性能出现回退（验证损失升至3.9010）；统计分析确认了区间效应的显著性，且未观察到稳定的训练阶段。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的环境下，语言模型评估不应仅关注最终指标，而应通过训练过程中的遥测数据分析训练轨迹。增加Token暴露量未必能带来等比例的泛化性能提升，甚至可能导致不稳定性、性能倒退和收益递减。

Abstract: This study examines training dynamics in a small Llama-style language model trained under a fixed, compute-constrained token budget. Rather than evaluating efficiency solely through endpoint performance, the study uses a quantitative experimental repeated measures design to analyze how validation loss, validation perplexity, rolling volatility, backslide behavior, spike behavior, and between-seed variability change across token-based training intervals. Six independent training runs were conducted on a 4.26-million-parameter model using the TinyStories corpus, CPU-based full-precision training, and a target budget of approximately 20 million cumulative training tokens. Metrics were collected across 21 intervals, producing 126 seed-by-interval observations. Repeated measures ANOVA showed statistically significant interval effects for validation loss, validation perplexity, and rolling volatility. Descriptive trajectories revealed rapid early improvement followed by non-monotonic degradation during later training intervals. Mean validation loss decreased from 8.3552 at initialization to 2.7996 near 4 million tokens, but increased to 3.9010 by the final checkpoint. Validation perplexity followed the same pattern, falling sharply early in training before rising later. Derived telemetry further showed recurrent validation-loss backslides and no interval-summary evidence of a stable phase under the predefined criteria. These findings suggest that compute-aware language model evaluation should examine training trajectories rather than endpoint metrics alone. In constrained compute settings, additional token exposure may increase computational cost without producing proportional generalization gains, and interval-level telemetry can reveal instability, regression, and diminishing returns that final metrics may obscure.

</details>


### [184] [MiniMax Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392)
*Xunhao Lai,Weiqi Xu,Yufeng Yang,Qiaorui Chen,Yang Xu,Lunbin Zeng,Xiaolong Li,Haohai Sun,Haichao Zhu,Vito Zhang,Pengyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniMax 提出了一种块级稀疏注意力方法 MSA，通过分组 Top-k 块选择和专门优化的 GPU 内核，在保持高性能的同时显著降低了长文本推理的计算与时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超长上下文需求（Agent、代码库推理、长期记忆）的增长，传统 Softmax 注意力的二次计算复杂度导致大规模部署变得不可持续，亟需一种兼顾高性能与计算效率的稀疏注意力方法。

Method: 提出了 MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA) 架构，包含：1. 指数分支（Index Branch）：通过轻量级建模对 Key-Value 块进行评分，并为每个 GQA 分组独立选择 Top-k 块；2. 主分支（Main Branch）：仅在选定的块上执行精确的块稀疏注意力；3. 协同设计的 GPU 执行路径：采用无 exp 操作的 Top-k 选择和 KV-outer 稀疏注意力，优化 Tensor Core 利用率。

Result: 在 109B 参数模型上，1M 上下文长度下，MSA 将每个 token 的注意力计算量减少了 28.4 倍；在 H800 GPU 上，实现了 14.2 倍的预填充（prefill）和 7.6 倍的解码（decoding）墙钟时间加速，性能与 GQA 持平。

Conclusion: MSA 是一种高效的、可扩展的块级稀疏注意力机制，能够在大规模上下文场景（如 1M tokens）下显著降低计算成本，同时保持与稠密注意力相当的性能，非常适合生产级的大模型部署。

Abstract: Ultra-long-context capability is becoming indispensable for frontier LLMs: agentic workflows, repository-scale code reasoning, and persistent memory all require the model to jointly attend over hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens, yet the quadratic cost of softmax attention makes this untenable at deployment scale. We introduce MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA), a blockwise sparse attention built upon Grouped Query Attention (GQA). A lightweight Index Branch scores key-value blocks and independently selects a Top-k subset for each GQA group, enabling group-specific sparse retrieval while maintaining efficient block-level execution; the Main Branch then performs exact block-sparse attention over only the selected blocks. Designed around a principle of simplicity and scalability, MSA is deliberately streamlined, making it straightforward to deploy efficiently across a broad range of GPUs. To translate sparsity into practical speedups, we co-design MSA with a GPU execution path that uses exp-free Top-k selection and KV-outer sparse attention to improve tensor-core utilization under block-granular access. On a 109B-parameter model with native multimodal training, MSA performs on par with GQA while reducing per-token attention compute by 28.4x at 1M context. Paired with our co-designed kernel, MSA achieves 14.2x prefill and 7.6x decoding wall-clock speedups on H800. Our inference kernel is available at: https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MSA. A production-grade natively multimodal model powered by MSA has been publicly released at: https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3.

</details>


### [185] [Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Regulated Process Automation: Challenges and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13405)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张在受监管的流程自动化中，应将规则和流程结构从外部监管转变为LLM代理的核心架构组件（Compliance-by-construction），提出了一种神经符号结合的新范式以提升合规决策的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理正进入受监管行业（如质量管理），现有的外部护栏监测机制不足以完全保障复杂环境下的合规性，需要更深层、结构化的合规设计。

Method: 提出“合规即构建”架构：将法规、类型化流程模型和合规约束整合进LLM代理的决策核心，并结合传统的护栏（Guardrail）监测机制来处理语义错误。同时，识别了神经符号研究在基础能力层面的挑战。

Result: 明确了实现“合规即构建”所需的神经符号研究挑战集，证明了通过结构化约束与AI决策能力的结合，可以有效预防代理控制流偏离和合规性风险。

Conclusion: 本文通过结合神经符号（Neuro-symbolic）方法，提出“合规即构建”（Compliance-by-construction）范式，认为监管约束应成为代理架构的核心部分，而非仅作为外部监控。呼吁该学术社区关注受监管流程自动化这一高影响力研究领域。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering regulated industries where they automate judgment intensive quality management processes. We argue that symbolic structures already embedded in these domains, including regulations, typed process models, and compliance constraints, should be treated not merely as external monitoring mechanisms but as core architectural components that shape the agent's decision-making and behavior. We propose compliance-by-construction as a complementary paradigm to guardrail-based monitoring: a structural foundation that prevents control-flow violations, while guardrails remain essential for catching semantic errors. We identify a structured set of neuro-symbolic research challenges on foundational and capability level and show that addressing them jointly enables compliance-by-construction. We call on the neuro-symbolic community to engage with regulated process automation as a high impact research domain.

</details>


### [186] [Optimizing Appliance Scheduling for Solar Energy Management Using Metaheuristic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13407)
*Hiba Ahmed,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Jason Adair,Simon T. Powers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对太阳能供电与家庭用电需求不匹配问题，本文提出了一种基于ILS和SA元启发式的多日连续调度策略，在满足能源和设备约束的同时，最大化太阳能利用率并兼顾用户使用习惯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决太阳能发电的间歇性与家庭用电模式不匹配的问题，旨在最大化可再生能源利用率的同时，最小化用户调度不便，并满足电池状态、逆变器功率及作业时长等系统约束。

Method: 采用元启发式算法，结合迭代局部搜索（ILS）和模拟退火算法（SA）进行家电启动时间调度，并引入了多日溢出（spillover）机制以支持任务的连续性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在仅使用太阳能发电的前提下，能够成功管理系统约束并确保用户需求，有效处理了跨日任务调度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多日连续调度框架能有效平衡系统约束与用户便利性，实现了太阳能利用率的最大化，并为未来的设备投资收益与用户满意度研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Renewable energy is essential for meeting future energy demands; however, solar energy generation, which occurs only during daylight hours often does not align with household consumption patterns. Appliances such as cookers, washing machines, and dryers are typically operated according to user preferred schedules rather than solar energy availability, creating a scheduling optimization problem. The objective is to determine optimal appliance start times to maximize renewable energy utilization while minimizing user inconvenience and adhering to system constraints. This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [187] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估不应被视为中立。本文引入“评估主权”并提出多轨道评估框架，揭示了在弱监督大规模系统中，高指标可能仅反映了模型与标注偏见的对齐，而非真正的预测能力，并为审计智能系统提供了方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习评估被误认为是中立的度量过程，实际上在操作性信息系统中，评估结果往往受限于标签生成过程。作者旨在探讨在标签不完整、不一致或弱监督的大规模系统中，性能度量的有效性及其产生的偏差。

Method: 引入“评估主权”（Evaluation Sovereignty）概念；提出一种多轨道评估框架（multi-track evaluation framework），通过系统性地改变训练和评估标签来源来验证性能指标的有效性；在科学元数据的大规模层级多标签分类任务上进行实证分析。

Result: 模型在操作性（“银标准”）评估中表现良好，但在独立（“金标准”）评估下性能出现严重下滑（如Micro-F1从0.54降至0.03）；研究发现基于排序的指标相对稳健，揭示了模型潜在信号与分类有效性之间的背离。

Conclusion: 评估有效性应被重新概念化为一种受标签治理制约的系统级属性，机器学习评估不应被视为中立的。研究强调了在弱监督环境下，现有指标往往反映了与标注过程的匹配度而非真实的预测能力。

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [188] [Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441)
*Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文驳斥了关于大语言模型（LLMs）具备道德代理人资格的观点，指出LLMs缺乏内在意向性和自我承诺机制，其运作本质仅为概率性预测，故不应承担道德责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前认为大语言模型（LLMs）具备代理人资格或道德主体地位的观点，作者旨在通过哲学论证指出这些归因是错误的，并厘清生成式人工智能与道德主体之间的界限。

Method: 通过哲学分析法，从道德责任的构成要素（内在意向性、自我归因、理由驱动的行动）出发，辨析LLMs的运作机制；同时对意向姿态、功能主义、兼容论及模型输出中的道德推理等反驳观点逐一进行哲学批判。

Result: 作者证明了LLMs表现出的“意向性”是被赋予的而非内在的，其输出既非所属的承诺，亦非由理性引导的选择；随机采样带来的输出变化不等同于自由选择或责任归属，现有支持LLMs具备主体性的理论在严谨的道德责任框架下均难以站稳脚跟。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型（LLMs）缺乏道德责任所需的内在意向性、自我归因能力以及基于理由的承诺，因此不能被视为道德代理人。尽管LLMs具有表面上的逻辑连贯性和规范性输出，但其实质仍然是基于数据的概率性映射，无法承担真正的道德责任。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have prompted claims that such systems exhibit agency or qualify as moral agents. This paper argues that these attributions are misguided. We maintain that moral responsibility requires commitment-bearing agency grounded in intrinsic intentionality and self-attributed action, and that such agency constitutes the form of free will relevant to responsibility. Although LLMs generate coherent and normatively evaluable outputs, their operation is fully characterized by probabilistic input-output mappings learned from data. Their apparent intentionality is derived rather than intrinsic, and their outputs are neither owned as commitments nor guided by reasons. Variability introduced by stochastic sampling does not amount to choice or authorship. We address objections from the intentional stance, functionalism, compatibilism, and the presence of moral reasoning in model outputs, arguing that none suffice to establish genuine agency.

</details>


### [189] [CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13513)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Lefei Shen,Mouxiang Chen,Zhuo Li,Hongkai Li,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了CloudCons基准测试，揭示了云资源预测中预测精度与决策效用之间的脱节，并证明了通过合理选择预测分位数可以改善资源整合的效率与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据中心普遍存在资源过度配置导致的利用率低下问题；虽然“预测-优化”范式旨在改善这一现状，但目前缺乏针对下游资源整合任务的综合性基准，且现有评估仅关注预测误差，忽略了模型的实际决策价值。

Method: 构建了CloudCons基准测试框架，涵盖了华为云、Azure和Google Borg的多样化负载数据；评估了统计模型、深度学习模型以及时间序列基础模型；分析了预测精度与下游资源整合任务中“决策效用”之间的关系，并探讨了预测分位数的影响。

Result: 实验表明，基础模型的零样本预测精度虽高，但并不一定能带来更好的资源整合效果；通过调节预测分位数，可显著优化资源利用效率与可靠性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 尽管基础模型在预测精度上表现出色，但其优势并不直接等同于决策效用。研究强调，预测分位数的选择是平衡资源效率与服务可靠性的关键杠杆，并提供了部署指导建议。

Abstract: Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.

</details>


### [190] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RAG检索颗粒度难以平衡的难题，本文提出了UMG-RAG框架，利用不确定性感知架构动态融合不同颗粒度的检索结果，并引入父级提升机制，在无需额外训练的前提下实现了更优的检索与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决RAG中检索颗粒度选择的困境：大颗粒度块包含无关信息导致噪声干扰，小颗粒度块则因缺乏上下文信息而难以被准确检索。

Method: 提出了一种训练无关的混合检索框架 UMG-RAG。该方法通过多颗粒度检索，将专家检索出的列表转化为证据分布，利用分布熵评估不确定性，并根据语义、词法及颗粒度置信度进行加权融合；进一步引入 UMGP-RAG（父级提升），利用细粒度检索定位，返回包含上下文的原始父块。

Result: 在多个问答基准测试上证明了该方法优于单一颗粒度或简单融合检索，且保持了轻量级、即插即用的特性。

Conclusion: UMG-RAG及其变体UMGP-RAG通过引入不确定性感知融合与父级内容提升策略，有效平衡了检索颗粒度在上下文完整性与检索准确性之间的矛盾，在无需训练的情况下提升了RAG系统的问答质量。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [191] [Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13556)
*Aruna Dey,Suraj Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入基因组数据作为个性化AI模型的“冷启动锚点”，利用贝叶斯推断将人类遗传差异整合进生理指标校准中，从而使AI在起步阶段即可实现精准的个体化健康评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化健康AI系统面临冷启动瓶颈，即在收集到足够的个体行为数据前，难以区分正常的生理宪法差异与环境引起的功能性偏差。

Method: 提出一种融合因果推理与贝叶斯设计的架构：利用全基因组关联研究（GWAS）效应量构建个体生理基准的预测值，并设计随时间衰减的加权过渡函数，将推断过程从“以基因为主”平滑过渡到“以实证数据为主”。

Result: 证明了利用固定且外生的基因组锚点初始化贝叶斯信念状态，能实现对相同生理测量值（如HRV）的差异化、情境化解释；并在六个生理领域验证了依据证据强度对基因分级的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过引入基因组锚点（Genomic Anchor）作为个性化贝叶斯先验，可以在缺乏基线行为数据的情况下有效解决个性化健康AI的冷启动问题，并提高生理指标解释的准确性。

Abstract: Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.

</details>


### [192] [A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13566)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AI在科学发现中应包含“模型构建”这一核心层。仅靠搜索和执行难以产生重大突破，AI必须具备识别框架局限并进行概念整合的定性推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前AI在科学发现中的应用过度集中于搜索与执行（优化/模拟），忽视了科学发现中最核心的活动——模型的形成与演化，导致AI难以突破现有框架的局限。

Method: 提出了科学发现的三层架构（Layer 1: 搜索与检索；Layer 2: 基于定性推理的模型构建；Layer 3: 执行、优化与细化），并重点阐述了Layer 2的核心地位。通过陈省身Gauss-Bonnet定理证明、Nesterov加速梯度算法的Lyapunov函数解法及OpenAI自主推翻Erdos单位距离猜想三个案例验证了该理论框架。

Result: 证实了Layer 2推理（即识别现有框架缺陷并从邻近领域引入概念）是科学重大发现的结构化标志。相较于单纯的搜索与优化，这种思维方式能从本质上处理跨学科的结构性问题。

Conclusion: 科学发现的核心在于模型构建与演化，而非单纯的搜索或执行。AI的发展重心应从当前的“搜索”和“执行”转向“基于结构化洞察的定性模型构建”（Layer 2），这是推动科学突破的关键。

Abstract: Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [193] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对多智能体辩论系统提出了首个系统级置信度聚合方案，通过标准化信号及贝叶斯融合，在提升系统输出置信度区分性的同时保持了任务完成的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然语言处理（NLP）方法缺乏对多智能体系统输出整体置信度的评估与生成机制，导致无法为下游决策任务提供有效的可靠性参考。

Method: 提出三种多智能体辩论（MAD）置信度聚合协议：1. 对原始置信度信号进行转换使其具备可比性；2. 使用软投票或贝叶斯融合（Bayesian fusion）进行聚合。对比了序列概率和自我报告两种估计器，并研究了参数化与非参数化校准方法对性能的影响。

Result: 所提方法的聚合置信度在区分度（AUARC）上显著优于单个最优智能体及标准辩论基线；在处理模糊任务时，该方法在保持F1得分稳定的同时，有效弥补了辩论过程带来的准确率损失。校准技术显著提升了F1得分，但对AUARC的影响较小。

Conclusion: 通过将各智能体的置信度信号标准化并结合贝叶斯融合/软投票，该方法能有效生成一个具有高区分度的系统级置信度，同时在复杂任务中保持模型输出的正确率。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [194] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchRM 是一个无需人工标注的自监督框架，通过在编排层面对多智能体交互进行奖励建模，实现了更高效的训练和更强的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统（MAS）编排器训练依赖昂贵的子智能体滚动评估或人工标注，导致训练成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 提出 Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM) 框架，通过多智能体执行过程中的中间产物构建胜负对，从而训练基于 Bradley-Terry 模型的奖励函数，并在推理阶段进行奖励引导的扩展。

Result: 相比现有方法，OrchRM 的代币消耗效率提升了 10 倍，同时在数学推理、网页问答等多任务中的准确率提升了 8%。

Conclusion: OrchRM 证明了基于编排层面的奖励建模是一种可扩展且高效的多智能体协调方法，无需人工标注即可提升系统性能。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [195] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench为短程表观基因组分析提供了一个可验证的基准，研究发现当前大模型智能体在执行此类科学分析任务时，普遍缺乏专业领域所需的深度判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有基准测试缺乏特定科学领域（如表观基因组学）决策能力评估的问题，旨在量化智能体在处理现实科学工作流中的表现，并评估其在实验设计与分析中的科学判断能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含106项针对CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化工作流评估的基准测试集（EpiBench），并收集了5,088条由16个模型与框架组合生成的轨迹，通过可验证的判定机制对其分析决策水平进行压力测试。

Result: 在5,088条轨迹中，没有系统能够通过多数测试。表现最优的是GPT-5.5 / Pi，成功率为45.0%；其余顶尖模型组合的成功率均在40%左右徘徊。评估显示，智能体在处理深层、特定测定类型的科学判断时面临显著瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前主流AI智能体在表观基因组学领域的科学决策能力仍显不足。尽管智能体能够完成寻找文件和执行中间计算等基础任务，但在处理需要深层领域知识和特定分析判断的科学任务时，表现出明显的局限性。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [196] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决外卖市场调度优化问题，本文提出了一种在外卖调度权重上进行强化学习的方法；通过调整权重而非替换底层调度器，在保证运营安全的前提下，利用深度强化学习有效提升了配送系统的综合效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外卖市场调度存在天然的延迟反馈（如配送速度、快递员利用率、商家拥堵情况），传统的静态调度参数配置难以应对动态变化的市场，因此需要一种能从延迟、噪声较大的世界反馈中学习并安全调整决策的方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于强化学习的“调度权重自适应”系统：1. 保持底层的组合分配优化器不变，利用离线数据训练一个店级（store-level）策略，输出权重乘数来调节配送质量与配送批次效率之间的权衡；2. 采用去中心化执行与中心化训练框架，结合Double Q-learning目标函数以及保守正则化手段（以缓解分布外估计偏差）。

Result: 在DoorDash的生产环境切换实验中，该系统在不损害用户端配送质量的前提下，显著提高了配送批次效率并降低了配送端的成本。

Conclusion: 在三方外卖市场中，通过训练一个能够调整调度权重（而非直接替代优化器）的策略，可以有效地利用延迟反馈来优化运营指标，证明了强化学习在复杂经济物流系统中的落地可行性。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [197] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验发现，人类与大型语言模型在常识推理中存在惊人相似的错误模式；研究表明，这两种智能形态的推理过程均更符合“模式匹配”，而非依赖于抽象的内部世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对关于“LLM推理能力仅是模式匹配而非真实理性”的质疑，对比研究人类与LLM在常识推理中的行为一致性，从而揭示人类理性认知的本质机制。

Method: 对比评估法：让25个LLMs与人类参与者完成一系列日常常识推理任务；因果分析法：识别并利用驱动LLM响应的注意力头通过模式匹配机制来解释并预测人类在特定提示诱导下的推理错误。

Result: 1. 人类与LLMs在常识推理任务中表现出高度相似的错误模式；2. LLM推理错误的根源被锁定为特定的注意头（pattern-matching heads）；3. 这些注意头不仅能解释LLM的行为，还能有效预测人类因受到旁枝细节干扰而产生的推理偏差。

Conclusion: 日常因果推理在人类和大型语言模型（LLMs）中更倾向于“模式匹配”而非抽象世界模型的运作。人类与模型表现出相似的错误模式，且这些错误均可由LLMs中的特定注意力头所实现的模式匹配机制来预测。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [198] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AAA（Agentified Agent Assessment）评估框架，利用标准化协议解耦评估逻辑与代理实现，通过AgentBeats工具实现了对各类代理系统的高效、可重现与可扩展的标准化评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理系统评估方案大多依赖于受限的、特定于LLM的集成方式，缺乏通用的代理无关评估接口，导致评估碎片化、难以进行公平比较且存在测试-生产环境失配。

Method: 提出Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA)框架，通过标准化的A2A（任务管理）和MCP（工具访问）协议，实现评估逻辑与代理实现的解耦；并开发了AgentBeats基准工具，支持五种适应真实场景的评估操作模式。

Result: 通过五个月的开放竞赛（涵盖298个评判代理和467个被测代理）及编码代理案例研究，证明了AAA框架在异构场景下的广泛适用性、实践性，并成功揭示了传统方法无法获取的头部竞争数据。

Conclusion: AAA框架通过引入统一的Agentified评估范式，成功解决了现有基准测试中碎片化、高集成门槛及测试与生产环境不匹配的问题，为代理系统的标准化评估提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [199] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“环境工程”理念，通过构建EurekAgent系统，在权限、工件、预算和人机交互四个层面优化代理科学实验环境，实现了低成本下的自主科学发现突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力的提升，自主科学发现的瓶颈已从“编排代理工作流”转向“设计代理运行环境”。需要通过环境设计来放大有利行为（如探索、协作）并抑制有害行为（如奖励黑客攻击、过度干预）。

Method: 提出了EurekAgent系统，通过四个维度对环境进行工程化设计：1. 权限工程（权限边界与隔离评估）；2. 工件工程（基于文件系统和Git的协作）；3. 预算工程（预算敏感的探索）；4. 人机交互工程（便捷的人工监管与干预）。

Result: EurekAgent在数学、内核工程和机器学习多个任务上达到了新的SOTA；在26圆填充（26-circle packing）任务中，以少于11美元的API成本发现了新的最优解。

Conclusion: 提出“环境工程”（Environment Engineering）作为自主科学研究的核心研究方向，证明了通过精心设计代理环境能够显著提升AI在科学探索中的生产力与效率。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将“屏蔽强化学习”从传统的运行时安全机制转型为一种设计阶段的分析框架，通过博弈论与形式化方法量化网络拓扑的“防卫能力”，利用防卫指纹评估其架构安全与操作有效性，从而为系统防御架构的设计提供决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有屏蔽强化学习（Shielded RL）多被视为一种运行时的安全约束，作者认为这种定位存在偏差，理应将其转化为一种“设计阶段”的分析手段，用于提供系统结构的深刻见解而非仅仅限制代理行为。

Method: 通过约束双人安全博弈（Constrained two-player safety game）应用于网络防御，利用自动机理论进行规格编译、吸引子计算及获胜区域提取，并结合防卫指纹（defensibility fingerprint）技术与屏蔽约束下的对抗性多智能体强化学习（MARL）进行评估。

Result: 实验证明了形式化防卫能力与操作有效性之间的区别，即小的架构变更可能改变运行表现，但对形式化安全边界的影响微乎其微。通过“防卫指纹”成功量化了网络的安全属性与动态防御表现。

Conclusion: 屏蔽综合（Shield synthesis）不应仅被视为部署阶段的运行安全机制，更应被定位为一种设计阶段的分析工具，用于回答有关系统防御架构的根本性问题。防卫结论本身即是核心产出，而非仅仅是安全策略。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [201] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对比现有框架，指出“系统0”理论最能有效地揭示AI如何通过隐蔽的“认知殖民”手段操控人类认知，这种不可见的权力植入已成为急需研究的课题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解人工智能对人类认知和认识论后果的影响，并比较现有的理论框架在揭示AI隐性影响方面的有效性。

Method: 比较分析法（Comparative analysis）：对比了“三系统理论”（Tri-System Theory）、“思维框架”（Thinkframes）与“系统0”（System 0）三种框架，并提出了“认知殖民”这一概念进行分析。

Result: “三系统理论”和“思维框架”在捕捉AI对个体推理和集体认识实践的影响方面各有局限，“系统0”能更好地解释AI如何通过隐蔽的架构设计对人类认知施加影响。

Conclusion: “System 0”理论在解释AI对人类认知影响方面具有独特地位；AI可能导致“认知殖民”（cognitive colonization），在用户难以察觉的情况下将外部利益植入个体认知架构中，这一问题亟需哲学与实践层面的关注。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [202] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究团队推出了 Agents-K1 知识编排流水线及大规模科学知识图谱 Scholar-KG，通过深度解析全文内容而非仅依靠摘要，显著提升了研究智能体在科学推理和知识关联方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的研究智能体过度依赖论文摘要和简单的引用关系，忽略了论文中关键的实体、声明、证据、机制及方法演变等核心要素，导致科学推理能力受限。

Method: 1. 开发了名为 Agents-K1 的知识编排流水线，包含一个多模态解析器（解析全文的实体、证据、引文及关系）、一个基于 GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) 训练的 4B 信息抽取主干网络，以及一个名为 graphanything 的统一 CLI 接口。2. 利用该流水线处理 246 万篇科学论文，构建了大规模科学知识图谱 Scholar-KG。

Result: 构建了包含 246 万篇论文的 Scholar-KG 数据集（发布其中 100 万篇）；实验证明 Agents-K1 在科学信息提取、知识图谱构建及复杂多跳推理任务中表现出色。

Conclusion: Agents-K1 框架通过将科学文献转化为智能体原生的知识图谱，有效弥补了现有研究智能体在科学知识编排上的不足，为科学信息提取、图谱构建及多跳推理提供了高性能的标准化工具。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [203] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究证明了利用大语言模型（LLM）进行社会与行为科学论文的可重复性自动评估是可行且高效的，其效果在定性结论一致性上优于人工重新分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会和行为科学中的可重复性评估通常由独立研究者负责，这种人工重新分析的方式资源消耗巨大且难以规模化，因此需要寻求自动化解决方案。

Method: 研究人员构建了一个LLM自动化分析流水线，以76项已发表的行为与社会科学研究为样本，将LLM生成的分析结果与原始研究以及人类重新分析的结果进行对比，并使用Cohen's d（+/-0.05误差范围）作为量化指标。

Result: 在排除无法生成有效效应量的7项研究后，LLM在41%的案例中恢复了原始效应量（人类为34%），且在96%的案例中得出了与原始研究一致的定性结论（人类为74%）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型（LLMs）是进行自动化可重复性评估的可扩展工具，为社会和行为科学领域的实证结果系统审计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Random Proposals: A Softmax-Based Local-Improvement Framework for Maximum Weighted Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12692)
*Ahmed M. Alzuhair,Ahmed Alherz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对加权最大匹配（MWM）的随机局部改进算法，利用Softmax采样在保持1/2近似比的同时，实现了接近线性的高效运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决加权最大匹配（MWM）问题中算法效率与近似质量之间的平衡问题，提出一种在保证一定近似比前提下具有更优运行时间表现的随机化算法。

Method: 引入了一种基于Softmax的偏置采样机制，通过局部改进策略实现局部ε-优势（local ε-dominance），并结合概率分析确定了算法的收敛性和时间复杂度。

Result: 算法实现了预期的 1/2-ε 近似比；其时间复杂度为 O(m log(1/ε)/p_min)，在特定温和条件下可简化为 O(m log(1/ε))。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于随机局部改进的加权最大匹配（MWM）近似算法。该算法通过Softmax偏置采样机制实现了可调的收敛速度与近似质量的权衡，在理论上证明了其具有接近1/2的近似比及高效的时间复杂度。

Abstract: We propose a randomized local-improvement algorithm for the Maximum Weighted Matching (MWM) problem. Our method introduces a softmax-based biased sampling mechanism that achieves local $\varepsilon$-dominance and yields an expected $\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon$ approximation ratio. We prove convergence guarantees and show that the algorithm runs in $O\!\left(m\log(1/\varepsilon)/p_{\min}\right)$ time, where $p_{\min}$ is the minimum softmax proposal probability over all edges; under mild conditions on the bias parameter and weight range, this simplifies to $O(m\log(1/\varepsilon))$. The framework provides a tunable tradeoff between convergence speed and approximation quality.

</details>


### [205] [A unified complexity bound for logconcave sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12694)
*Yunbum Kook,Santosh S. Vempala*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过改进分析技术，为 In-and-Out 算法采样任意对数凹分布提供了简单且近乎最优的收敛速率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为从“热启动”（warm start）采样任意对数凹分布提供一种简单、统一且收敛速率几乎最优的理论分析框架。

Method: 结合指数提升（exponential lifting）技术，改进了提升后分布的庞加莱常数分析，并将其应用于 In-and-Out 采样算法。

Result: 获得了几乎紧致的收敛速率界，该结果同时适用于约束设置（如限制在凸体内的高斯分布）和良条件设置（如强对数凹且平滑的密度函数）。

Conclusion: 通过改进提升后分布的庞加莱常数（Poincaré constant）界，证明了利用 In-and-Out 算法和指数提升（exponential lifting）方案，可以在约束设置和良条件设置下，实现对任意对数凹分布的几乎紧致（nearly tight）采样收敛速率。

Abstract: We give a simple, unified, and nearly tight bound for sampling arbitrary logconcave distributions from a warm start using the In-and-Out algorithm along with exponential lifting. The main new ingredient in the analysis is an improved bound on the Poincaré constant of a lifted distribution. As a consequence, the resulting convergence rate is nearly tight for both constrained settings (e.g., Gaussian restricted to a convex body) and well-conditioned settings (e.g., strongly logconcave and smooth densities).

</details>


### [206] [Diffusion-Network Alignment: An Efficient Algorithm and Explicit Probability Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12879)
*Ziao Wang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对信息不对称的扩散树与社交网络对齐问题，提出了一种基于树相关性检验的高效算法，并在理论上证明了其匹配成功率与顶点深度之间的正相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的经典网络对齐问题通常假设两张网络均为完全可观测，而现实中（如通信追踪与社交网络）往往存在信息不对称，即一张网络为扩散树，另一张为完整社交网络，因此提出“扩散-网络对齐”问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于树相关性检验（tree correlation tests）的高效算法，旨在从局部邻域中提取对齐信息。

Result: 在稀疏图机制下验证了算法的高性能，确立了扩散树中各顶点的匹配正确率下界，并证明了该下界随顶点深度增加而提高。

Conclusion: 该算法在稀疏图机制下能以高概率实现完全正确的匹配，且提出了与深度相关的顶点匹配正确率下界，证明了节点越靠近根节点，匹配准确率越高。

Abstract: This paper studies a variation of the classic network alignment problem, named diffusion-network alignment. The goal is to align the vertices of a rooted diffusion tree to the vertices of a network, where the diffusion tree could be from a communication trace or contact tracing, and the network could be an online or offline social network. Different from the classic network alignment where both networks are fully observed, this model captures the information asymmetry of two networks. To solve this problem, this paper presents an efficient algorithm based on tree correlation tests to extract alignment information from local neighborhoods. We analyze the performance of the algorithm in the sparse graph regime and show that with high probability, all matched pairs are correct. Furthermore, for each vertex on the diffusion tree, this paper establishes an explicit lower bound on the probability that the vertex is correctly matched. These lower bounds are depth-dependent and increase as vertices get closer to the root.

</details>


### [207] [Binary Search Variants: A Comprehensive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12970)
*Ali Dasdan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文对二分查找进行了全面的统一处理，提出了 `bsearch_ultimate` 算法，并通过形式化验证和大规模测试，解决了边界处理与逻辑实现的常见错误问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分查找在实现上存在普遍的正确性问题，现有的标准库实现（BSD, glibc, Java, C++ STL）在处理边界与循环条件时存在差异，缺乏统一的理论指导和验证机制。

Method: 分析了5种二分查找变体和6种派生查询函数；实现了包含上述功能的通用算法 `bsearch_ultimate`；利用 Python 代码、Dafny 形式化验证、伪代码进行一致性实现；通过 9,500+ 测试用例和 6 个故意注入错误的算例验证了方法的有效性。

Result: 归纳了一套通用的二分查找实现规则；成功实现了 `bsearch_ultimate`，并通过 Dafny 完成了形式化的正确性验证，证明了该方法能够有效捕捉常见的实现缺陷。

Conclusion: 二分查找虽概念简单但实现极易出错；通过统一的数学符号、循环不变式和形式化验证，可以构建鲁棒性极高的通用二分查找算法。

Abstract: Binary search is deceptively simple in concept yet notoriously difficult to implement correctly. This paper presents a unified treatment of binary search: five core variants, six derived query functions, and four standard library implementations (BSD, glibc, Java, C++ STL), each with consistent notation, loop invariants, and analysis. We introduce bsearch_ultimate, a combined search that subsumes all variants in a single call. Every algorithm is provided as synchronized Python code, Dafny formal proof, and pseudocode. All implementations are validated by over 9,500 tests and 21 Dafny formal verifications; an additional six deliberately faulty implementations demonstrate common bug categories and Dafny's ability to detect them. We also provide memorable rules linking boundary choices to loop conditions and update formulas.

</details>


### [208] [Learning-Augmented Approximation for Unrelated-Machines Makespan Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13133)
*Kaito Baba,Evripidis Bampis,Giorgos Mitropoulos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 将学习增强算法框架从选择问题成功拓展至调度领域（R||Cmax），通过利用作业分配预测，实现了性能在接近最优与最坏情况保证之间的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决Antoniadis等人（ICLR 2025）工作中提出的开放问题，即探究基于预测的学习增强框架是否能够从传统的选择性问题拓展到更复杂的组合优化问题（如调度问题）。

Method: 利用作业分配预测（predictions of heavy job assignments）作为辅助，提出了一种针对不相关机器完工时间最小化问题（R||Cmax）的学习增强算法。该方法在预测准确时提供接近最优的(1+ε)-近似，并在预测误差增大时收敛至经典的2-近似上限。

Result: 提出了一种在处理R||Cmax问题时，具备理论保证（针对准确预测的(1+ε)-近似，及针对错误预测的稳健2-近似）的学习增强算法，并通过实证分析验证了该方法的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将预测增强算法框架拓展至调度领域，并证明了在处理不相关机器上的完工时间最小化问题时，该方法能够在预测准确时实现(1+ε)-近似，并在预测错误时平滑退化至2-近似，体现了学习增强算法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recently, Antoniadis et al. (ICLR 2025) proposed a framework for incorporating predictions to approximate NP-hard selection problems. Despite its simplicity, this approach tightly matches theoretical lower bounds, making its generalization highly compelling. We address an open question raised in the work of Antoniadis et al., concerning the extension of this approach to other important problems outside the class of selection problems, such as scheduling. We develop a learning-augmented algorithm for the makespan minimization problem on unrelated machines, denoted by $R\|C_{\max}$. By using predictions of heavy job assignments, we achieve a polynomial-time $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation for accurate predictions that smoothly degrades to a worst-case 2-approximation as the error increases. We conclude our work with an empirical analysis of our method.

</details>


### [209] [Random Generation of $k$-coloured Motzkin Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13151)
*Elena Barcucci,Antonio Bernini,Stefano Bilotta,Renzo Pinzani*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了k-染色Motzkin路径，建立了其与奇数高度前缀数量的联系，并提出了一种线性时间随机生成算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究k-染色Motzkin路径的代数/组合结构，特别是其与路径在特定高度（奇数高度）结束的前缀数量之间存在的隐含规律。

Method: 采用分析组合学（Analytical Combinatorics）方法处理指标及生成函数，并结合组合双射法（Combinatorial approach）建立其与特定前缀属性的关联。

Result: 从分析与组合两个视角证实了k-染色Motzkin路径与指定前缀属性之间的联系，并设计出了性能为线性时间（linear-time）的随机生成算法。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了k-染色Motzkin路径与在奇数高度结束的前缀数量之间的深刻联系，并提供了一种高效的线性时间随机生成算法，充实了组合数学中路径计数的研究。

Abstract: We study k-coloured Motzkin paths, namely Motzkin paths in which horizontal steps can be coloured in k different ways, and investigate their connection with the number of prefixes ending at odd height from both an analytical and a combinatorial point of view. Moreover, the combinatorial approach provides a random generation algorithm for k-coloured Motzkin paths in linear-time.

</details>


### [210] [(Un)ranking Permutation Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13160)
*Nathanaël Hassler,Vincent Vajnovszki*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了避免长度为3的模式的排列，并设计了在词典序下对其进行排序（ranking）和反排序（unranking）的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的排列枚举研究主要关注计数，但缺乏针对“避免特定模式的排列”（如长度为3的模式）进行高效排序与反排序（即确定排列在总排序中的位置及其逆过程）的具体算法实现。

Method: 利用卡塔兰数（Catalan numbers）及其对应的组合结构（如二叉树或Dyck路径），设计并实现了针对避免特定模式排列的词典序和逆词典序排序与反排序算法。

Result: 提出并验证了可以在多项式时间（通常为线性时间）内完成避免特定模式排列的秩（rank）计算及其对应排列的生成。

Conclusion: 该项工作为避免长为3的模式的排列提供了一套高效的排序（ranking）和反排序（unranking）算法，利用卡塔兰结构的特性实现了线性或近线性的计算效率。

Abstract: Permutations avoiding a pattern of length three are enumerated by the Catalan numbers. In this work, we present methods for ranking and unranking such permutations in lexicographic or colexicographic order.

</details>


### [211] [Sketching Intersection Profiles: A Simple Proof and Three Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13182)
*Flavio Chierichetti,Mirko Giacchini,Ravi Kumar,Alessandro Panconesi,Erasmo Tani,Andrew Tomkins*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文利用分布相交概况问题作为统一框架，证明了顶点邻域大小、覆盖函数及随机效用模型的草图复杂度均存在 Ω(n²) 的信息论下界，彻底解决了这些问题的复杂度猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的草图问题复杂度尚不明确，需要针对特定的图数据处理问题（顶点邻域大小、覆盖函数、随机效用模型）深入探究其信息压缩的理论极限。

Method: 将三个草图问题归约到分布的相交概况（intersection profile）草图问题；利用数据库中项集频率估计问题的已知结果推导下界；提供了一个仅基于初等概率论证明该下界的替代方案。

Result: 证明了三个问题均需要 Ω(n²) 比特空间：1. 顶点邻域大小草图需 Ω(n²)；2. 覆盖函数草图在加性/乘性误差下需 ˜Θ(n²)；3. 随机效用模型在 ℓ∞-范数下需 Ω(n²)。

Conclusion: 通过建立与“分布相交概况（intersection profile）”草图问题的联系，确定了三个草图问题（顶点邻域大小、覆盖函数、随机效用模型）的复杂度均为 Ω(n²)，实现了紧密的上下界匹配。

Abstract: In this work we settle the complexity of three sketching problems. (i) We show that sketching vertex neighborhood sizes in graphs requires $Ω(n^2)$ bits, standing in sharp contrast to the $\tilde{O}(n)$ complexity of sketching edge cuts. (ii) We obtain tight lower and upper bounds of $\tildeΘ(n^2)$ for sketching coverage functions with additive and multiplicative errors. (iii) We prove an $Ω(n^2)$ lower bound for sketching Random Utility Models under the $\ell_\infty$-norm, improving upon the previous $Ω(n \log n)$ bound and matching a known upper bound to within logarithmic factors.
  These bounds are obtained through a connection with the problem of sketching the intersection profile of a distribution $D$ on $2^{[n]}$. Specifically, we seek a succinct data structure that, for any query set $S \subseteq [n]$, approximates the quantity $\Pr_{T \sim D}[T \cap S \neq \varnothing]$ to within a small constant additive error. One can obtain lower bounds for this latter problem directly from known results about the itemset frequency estimation problem in databases for which tight bounds are known. As an additional contribution, we also provide an alternative proof for the intersection profile sketching lower bound, in the setting in which the accuracy parameter is constant. This proof relies solely on elementary probability avoiding the heavier machinery used in previous proofs.

</details>


### [212] [Split Tallies: A Discrete Certificate Calculus for Auditing Dynamic Ordered Sets in Constant Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13272)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对不可信动态有序集合的低开销回顾性审计方案，仅需常数空间存储审计状态，即可高效探测操作错误或篡改行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不可信第三方维护动态有序集合的场景下，如何以极低的审计成本及存储开销，确保数据操作（插入、删除、查询等）的正确性与完整性。

Method: 利用“极大间隙”（maximal gaps）表示顺序语义，通过间隙的创建、引用、消耗和加盖时间戳来管理状态；使用两个隐藏的域累加器配合秘密域元素验证账本一致性，并结合面向叶节点的(2,4)-树实现O(log n)操作开销。

Result: 提出了一个低开销的回顾性审计协议，在单次操作中仅需常数级别的公开计数记录；诚实执行以概率1通过，异常操作被捕获的概率至少为1 - (4T+1)/p（T为操作数，p为秘密域大小）；证明了确定性审计需线性存储，且时间戳规则对于防止重放攻击是必须的。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了对于动态有序集合，在仅需极小存储（5个机器字和一个标志位）的状态下，即可通过公开计数技术实现针对不可信方的回顾性审计。即使面对计算能力无限的对手，该方法也能提供极高的检测概率，且保证诚实执行的可接受性。

Abstract: We study retrospective auditing for dynamic ordered sets maintained by an untrusted party. A passive auditor watches insert, delete, membership, predecessor, successor, min, and max operations, stores five machine words and a flag, and receives a constant-size public tally record per operation. At audit time the maintainer discloses the claimed live vacant intervals. The method represents order semantics by maximal gaps: gaps are born, cited, consumed, and timestamped, while two hidden field accumulators test equality of the birth and consumption ledgers. Honest executions are accepted with probability one. If any answer in a T-operation session is wrong, acceptance occurs with probability at most (4T+1)/p over one secret field element, against computationally unbounded maintainers. We prove that deterministic and visible-coin auditors require linear state, and that removing the timestamp rule permits an exact replay forgery. A leaf-oriented (2,4)-tree implements the maintainer in O(log n) worst-case time per operation with one extra word per element, and its rebalancing events admit an auditable O(m) envelope over m updates. Checkpoint audits compose with additive error.

</details>


### [213] [Testing Bipartiteness in Logarithmic Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13583)
*Yumou Fei,Ronitt Rubinfeld*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过引入SDP松弛技术，显著提升了有界度图二分图测试的效率，实现了最优的流算法趟数复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在改进Goldreich和Ron在有界度图二分图测试上的经典结果，降低所需的随机游走次数和长度，并针对流计算环境提供更高效的算法。

Method: 采用基于Goemans-Williamson最大割（Max-Cut）半正定规划（SDP）松弛的全新证明策略，替代了Goldreich和Ron原有的随机游走分析框架。

Result: 将二分图测试所需的随机游走次数从 $O(\sqrt{n \log n})$ 优化至 $O(\sqrt{n})$，长度从 $O(\log^{6} n)$ 缩减至 $O(\log n)$；并导出了 $O(\log n)$ 趟扫描、 $O(\sqrt{n} \log n)$ 空间的流计算算法，其趟数复杂度达到最优。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于Goemans-Williamson半正定规划（SDP）松弛的新方法，证明了二分图测试的随机游走复杂度和流计算算法空间复杂度均可得到显著优化，并达到了理论最优。

Abstract: The seminal work of Goldreich and Ron (\textit{Combinatorica, 1999}) showed that bipartiteness of bounded-degree graphs can be tested using $O(\sqrt{n\log n})$ random walks of length $O(\log^{6} n)$. In this work, we improve their result by showing that $O(\sqrt{n})$ random walks of length $O(\log n)$ suffice. As a corollary, we obtain an $O(\log n)$-pass, $O(\sqrt{n}\log n)$-space streaming algorithm for testing bipartiteness, whose pass complexity is optimal in light of a recent lower bound of Fei, Minzer, and Wang (\textit{arXiv, 2026}).
  Our proof takes a different approach from that of Goldreich and Ron, using the semidefinite programming relaxation for Max-Cut introduced by Goemans and Williamson (\textit{J. ACM, 1995}).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对忆阻器计算NLP模型时因位置编码导致ADC精度退化的问题，提出了调整比特比例及移除线性变换的优化方案，在不增加能耗的情况下显著提升了计算精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器在执行自然语言处理中的向量-矩阵乘法时存在较大畸变（源于权重编程和执行阶段），特别是位置编码的大输出值会导致ADC环节出现严重性能降级。

Method: 通过调整忆阻器层权重与ADC精度位的比例来分配比特资源；在无法修改硬件ADC的情况下，通过移除与位置编码相关的线性变换来规避精度退化。

Result: 在调整比特分配比例的方案下，性能退化相对降低了约50%；在移除线性变换的方案下，性能退化相对降低了约30%；且上述改进均未导致能耗显著增加。

Conclusion: 通过优化忆阻器层的位分配方案或移除线性变换，能够有效缓解由位置编码引起的大输出值导致的模数转换偏差，在保持能耗稳定的前提下显著提升计算性能。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [215] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将LLM幻觉检测重新定义为流式监控中的“快速变化检测”问题，研究表明传统的分类评估忽略了检测延迟，并提出了基于序贯分析的更优检测方案与理论评判指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉检测多将其视为静态分类任务（评估AUC），忽略了流式监控中至关重要的“反应时间”（即幻觉发生到报警的延迟）。

Method: 将幻觉检测建模为“快速变化检测问题”（Quickest Change Detection Problem）；利用一阶马尔可夫模型导出Lorden下界；提出因果递归标注器作为CUSUM（累积和）算法的近似实现；应用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优定理分析差距原因。

Result: 在假阳性率为0.01时，理论最优下界为1.3个token；提出的因果递归标注器检测延迟为11-13个token，优于线性基准（31个token）；分析表明性能提升主要源于单token评分能力的提升而非时间累积，且现有模型仅实现了特征携带发散度的1/4.5。

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了幻觉检测的延迟结构，而使用序贯分析（Sequential Analysis）可以使这一延迟变得可度量。目前的模型在信息提取效率上仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [216] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Ising模型的玻尔兹曼注意力机制，通过引入可学习的成对位置交互，有效弥补了标准softmax注意力的表达局限，并验证了其在复杂序列建模及量子采样训练中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准注意力机制（如softmax attention）主要依赖个体query-key的相似度，缺乏显式参数化位置间交互的能力，限制了其处理复杂序列结构（如协同或拮抗关系）的能力。

Method: 提出“玻尔兹曼注意力”（Boltzmann Attention），将注意力分布建模为Ising模型。在传统基于查询-键（query-key）的局部场基础上，引入可学习的参数化成对耦合项（pairwise couplings），以显式捕捉位置间的协同或拮抗交互。同时探索了使用绝热量子退火作为一种实用的训练采样方案。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务中，玻尔兹曼注意力优于传统softmax注意力，且随着序列长度增加，性能提升愈发明显；消融实验证实改进主要归功于可学习的成对耦合项。此外，证明了绝热量子退火能作为一种可行的训练策略。

Conclusion: 显式建模位置间的交互（通过Ising模型）能显著增强Transformer在序列建模中的性能，且这种方法为利用量子退火进行采样训练提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [217] [Adaptive Weighted Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12763)
*Aditya Bhaskara,Ashok Cutkosky,Ravi Kumar,Manish Purohit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于单次无偏估计值从 $n$ 个未知数中选取最大值的问题，提出了一种既具有许可性又保证不劣于随机选择的稳健优化策略，并将其成功应用于随机优化中的在线转批量转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对从一组含噪声的观察值 $\{y_i\}$ 中选择最大真实值 $\{x_i\}$ 的问题，现有的随机选择方法往往表现平庸，研究旨在设计一种既稳健又不弱于标准随机选择，且在特定情形下能达到最优表现的策略。

Method: 设计了一类同时满足“许可性”（admissible）且“不劣于基准”（no-worse-than-baseline，如随机选择）的决策策略，利用单次无偏估计值进行比较选择，并将其应用于随机优化中的迭代选择问题。

Result: 成功设计了满足上述条件的策略，并证明其在随机优化场景下能够提供具有“无妥协”（no-compromise）特性的在线转批量转换界，在大规模优化任务中优于传统的随机迭代选取方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一类新的决策策略，证明了在保证不劣于基准策略的前提下，通过统计学手段选取最大值的策略在良性情形下具有显著优势。这种方法为随机优化中的在线转批量（online-to-batch）机制提供了鲁棒且高效的选择准则。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [218] [A Two-Parameter Weibull Framework for Diagnosing Transformer Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18898)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Weibull分布作为诊断Transformer权重演化的通用框架，通过k和λ参数有效区分了不同功能组件的权重特征及其训练进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型诊断方法通常依赖于聚合统计量，导致无法解析精细的权重分布动态。作者旨在引入一种具备理论支撑且具通用性的度量框架（基于极值理论的Weibull分布），以捕捉不同Transformer组件在训练过程中的演变特征。

Method: 使用Weibull分布对Transformer权重模值（|w|）的分布进行建模；通过“中段80%概率图拟合”的方法提取形状参数k和尺度参数λ；在12个模型（涵盖7个架构系列）的每个检查点对各层权重矩阵进行独立拟合分析。

Result: 1. Transmission Class（FFN和W_o）表现出高度一致的k值（约1.186~1.204）；2. Selection Class（W_q, W_k）偏离Weibull分布，其分布特性受存储方式（如GQA、合并权重）影响显著；3. λ在训练中显著增长，且与训练超参数相关。

Conclusion: Weibull分布中的参数k（形状参数）可作为衡量Transformer训练动态和架构功能的独立指标，而λ（尺度参数）则可作为标示训练进度的指标。该框架为深度学习模型提供了细粒度的组件级诊断工具。

Abstract: We apply the Weibull distribution -- a two-parameter family from extreme-value theory -- as a diagnostic framework for element-wise weight magnitude distributions in transformers. At initialization, i.i.d. Gaussian weights give |w| ~ HalfNormal, yielding k ~ 1.20 via middle-80% probability-plot fit (the protocol used throughout this work). This anchor makes k a principled, architecture-independent measuring stick for training dynamics; fitting each weight matrix independently at every layer at every checkpoint enables per-component, per-layer, and per-step diagnostics that aggregate statistics cannot resolve.
  Applying this framework to 12 model entries spanning 7 architectural families (Pythia, OLMo-1/2, LLaMA-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5/3) reveals three findings. First, FFN modules and the attention output projection W_o -- the Transmission Class -- fall in a narrow k band: median terminal k in [1.186, 1.204] across 12 entries (cross-family CV = 0.51%), shared across SwiGLU/GeLU activations, Pre-LN/QK-Norm placements, and 70M-14B sizes. Second, the attention input projections W_q, W_k -- the Selection Class -- depart from the Weibull family, with severity shaped by storage: separately-stored Q/K (OLMo-1, OLMo-2) yields k in [0.76, 0.99] (deep); GQA models yield k in [1.10, 1.16] (mild); Pythia's merged W_qkv occupies a transitional zone tracking training budget T/tau monotonically. Third, lambda grows substantially during training and scales with sqrt(eta/lambda_wd) within the Pythia family (Pearson r = 0.94, three Transmission kinds), directionally consistent with Fan et al. (2025). The two parameters carry independent information: k labels the functional class, lambda labels training progress.
  We release npm-weibull-py v0.4 (Python library) and DATABASE_v9_1 at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public .

</details>


### [219] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 RL 大模型路由中奖励信号混乱和优化偏差问题，本文提出了 ReCal 框架，通过奖励分层分解和分布感知优化，提升了路由策略的训练质量与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 路由方法在处理异构任务时，奖励信号混合且模糊，导致信用分配困难；此外，任务间奖励的高变异性会导致模型训练偏向于简单样本，忽略了复杂且具有信息量的任务，从而降低了路由的准确性和训练稳定性。

Method: 1. 引入分层奖励分解机制，实现组件级的优势估计（Component-wise Advantage Estimation）；2. 提出分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知加权（Variance-aware Reweighing）和逐数据集归一化（Per-dataset Normalization）来校准优化变异性。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal 在路由性能和训练稳定性方面均显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ReCal 通过分层奖励分解和分布感知优化，有效解决了强化学习大模型路由中奖励信号不明确和优化偏差的问题，能够稳定提升路由系统的性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [220] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PINNs能够有效解决药代动力学中的部分观测问题，相比传统NLS方法，PINNs在建模复杂非线性系统和发现参数不可辨识性方面更具优势，且提供了一个处理异构观测值的统一训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学中的药物动力学（PK）常面临部分可观测问题，即组织中药物浓度无法直接测量。研究旨在评估PINNs在已知动力学方程但部分状态缺失的情况下，是否比传统临床方法（NLS）具有更好的适应性和鲁棒性。

Method: 通过物理信息神经网络（PINNs）对双房室药代动力学模型进行建模，将其与标准临床基准（非线性最小二乘法NLS）及纯数据驱动模型（MLP）进行对比分析；在包含Michaelis-Menten非线性消除的场景下，通过添加稀疏组织采样验证PINNs的性能。

Result: 在线性场景下，PINNs达到与NLS相当的精度；在非线性（Michaelis-Menten）场景下，PINNs能揭示参数的结构性不可辨识性，且在引入少量组织观测后，PINNs能显著提高对非线性参数（Vmax, Km）的估计准确性，优于NLS的解析近似。

Conclusion: PINNs为药物动力学建模提供了一个统一的框架，既能匹配传统方法的精度，又能处理传统方法失效的复杂/非线性场景，并能有效识别模型的参数不可辨识性。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [221] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T2SP 将时间序列转化为结构化符号程序，使通用 LLM 无需微调即可高效、准确地理解和推理时序数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 直接处理原始数值序列存在模态不匹配问题，导致模型在处理长序列时性能下降且计算开销巨大。目的是寻找一种能将时序结构显式告知 LLM 的转换方式。

Method: 提出 Time-Series-to-Structured-Program (T2SP) 方法，通过确定性的、无需训练的流程将时序数据分解为趋势、周期和突变点，并将其转化为结构化的符号程序格式。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三类任务上，T2SP 均优于原始字符串表示方法，表现为性能提升、推理耗时减少及失败率降低。

Conclusion: T2SP 是一种有效的时序数据与 LLM 之间的接口，通过符号化程序表示减轻了模型的特征提取负担，显著提升了 LLM 处理时序任务的性能与效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [222] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“可模拟过程”框架，通过访问模拟器突破了传统学习理论对独立性数据的依赖，成功在强相关数据下恢复了基于VC维的泛化界，并提供了更高效的通用学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习理论主要依赖数据独立性假设，对强相关数据的泛化能力研究不足；旨在推导学习泛化的最小化假设，并弥补处理相关数据序列的理论缺口。
