TL;DR: 本文分析了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的参数更新机制，发现其表现出独特的稀疏结构（主要在FFN层）和几何特征（避开主方向，针对零权重区），并指出AdamW在保持模型性能方面优于稀疏化优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation (OPD) 已成为主流的后期训练方法，但其结合了在线策略轨迹和稠密教师监管的混合机制如何改变模型参数尚不明确。

Method: 通过对多个语言模型和视觉语言模型对进行实验，分析了OPD更新的稀疏性（结构、层分布、对训练效率的影响）和几何特性（秩分布、奇异值谱、与源权重坐标的关系）。

Result: 1. 稀疏性：OPD更新在空间上是稀疏的且分布在各层（FFN为主），仅训练该子网络即可恢复大部分性能；但AdamW比稀疏SGD更适合此任务。2. 几何特性：更新虽然满秩但谱集中，避开了源权重的奇异值主方向，且倾向于更新源权重接近零的坐标。

Conclusion: OPD并非简单的稠密参数重写，其更新过程保留了在线策略训练的独特几何特征。虽然存在稀疏性（主要集中在FFN层），但利用AdamW的自适应标度对于处理异构梯度尺度至关重要，简单的稀疏优化器效果不佳。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [335] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络中截断式位置编码的理论表达能力，证明了截断会改变不同位置编码间的等价性，使其不再强于1-WL测试，并提出混合多种截断编码是实际应用中的更优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的位置编码理论等价性基于“完整版”编码，其计算代价过大（O(n^3)），而实际常用的“截断式”编码的理论性质尚不明确。

Method: 理论分析截断式位置编码的表达能力，对比谱位置编码与基于游走的位置编码；引入并研究k-调和距离；在真实数据集上进行实证对比。

Result: 证明截断后谱位置编码与基于游走的位置编码在表达能力上并不等价；证明截断谱位置编码不再强于1-WL测试；证明即便相似的截断式编码在表达能力上也存在显著差异；实证说明混合截断式编码效果更好。

Conclusion: 截断式位置编码在表达能力上存在本质差异，且截断会导致谱位置编码失去超越1-WL测试的能力。混合使用多种截断式位置编码在实践中表现更优。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [336] [Trait, Not State: The Durability of Reading Identity in Social Highlighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12904)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究通过长达两年的社交高亮数据追踪，证实了读者的“选择特征”具有高度的长期稳定性，更倾向于是一种稳定的个性“特质”而非随时间波动的“状态”，且具备极强的未来阅读预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究证明了“选择”可以体现个体独特性，但仅限于横截面分析。本研究旨在探讨这种选择特征在时间维度上的稳定性：它究竟是随时间变化的“状态”，还是相对固定的个体“特质”。

Method: 构建基于社交网络高亮数据的研究框架。将每位用户的前六个月高亮内容固定为“特征描述文件”（profile），通过引入同一日历时段内的对照组排除“供给漂移”（supply drift）干扰，在全局和兴趣邻域细粒度下，追踪其在长达24个月跨度内的选择表现。

Result: 1. 细粒度分析显示，用户选择特征在长达24个月内没有统计学意义上的显著下降；2. 这种预测能力并非简单重复之前阅读过的领域；3. 个体内在偏好演变缓慢；4. 预测准确率（AP）远高于任何非个性化的简单基准方法。

Conclusion: 读者的选择特征（Selection Signature）表现出高度的稳定性，应被视为一种类似“特质”（trait）而非瞬时“状态”（state）的属性。即便跨越两年时间，个人的历史高亮记录仍能强力预测其未来的阅读选择。

Abstract: Prior work on a social web highlighter located individuality in selection -- which documents a person chooses to highlight -- but measured it cross-sectionally. We ask the temporal question: is a reader's selection signature a trait or a state? We freeze each reader's first six months of highlighting as a profile and track its own-vs-other advantage on their later selections at growing gaps (to 24+ months), with negatives drawn from the same calendar era -- so supply drift cannot masquerade as personal drift -- at a coarse global level and at a fine level whose negatives and controls come from the reader's own interest neighborhood; the anchor cell reproduces the prior cross-sectional level (+0.188 vs +0.169), validating the harness. Four results. Within the same users, the fine-layer advantage shows no statistically detectable paired decline at any horizon (6-12 month retention R = 1.00 [0.85, 1.18], n = 212; the farthest bin is compatible with a modest decline; the only contrast whose interval excludes zero is the coarse layer at 12-24 months, about 13%). The signal is not reducible to repeated domains (~90% survives excluding all profile sources). Within-person drift is slow (a recent-half profile beats the old half by +0.042). Prospectively, personal profiles -- even one built from a reader's earliest documents, median 20 months before evaluation -- rank their next reads at roughly 3x the AP of every simple non-personal prior tested. We use "trait" operationally (a stable signature under continued engagement); the scope is heavy, long-tenured readers of one platform, and exposure is not separable from choice.

</details>


### [337] [Charge as a Construct-Validity Factor in Chinese Legal Case Retrieval: A Cross-Benchmark Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12993)
*Yao Liu,Tien-Ping Tan,Zhilan Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究指出中文法律案例检索基准测试存在“罪名泄露”导致的构念效度缺失，目前的高性能评分主要归因于任务构建中对“罪名匹配”的依赖，而非真正的推理能力。作者提出了CCE评估协议，旨在剔除这类偏差以更准确地衡量模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的法律案例检索（LCR）基准测试表现过高（NDCG@10达0.85-0.88），作者怀疑这些高分并非源于模型具备了法律推理能力，而是因为基准测试的设计本身高度依赖“罪名”这一特征，从而掩盖了系统在实际性能上的提升。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于“共享罪名+BM25”排序的简单启发式策略，对比当前主流检索系统；2. 使用宏观AUC分析罪名与相关性之间的关联强度；3. 在三个主流数据集（LeCaRDv2, LeCaRDv1, CAIL2022）上进行实验验证；4. 构建并开源了“电荷控制评估协议（CCE）”框架，用于检测模型的构造效度。

Result: 1. 在LeCaRDv2上，仅使用“共享罪名”排序即消除了BM25与最优模型之间99.2%的性能差距；2. 随着罪名与相关性之间关联强度（Macro-AUC）的减弱，该启发式策略在不同数据集上的有效性也相应下降；3. 现有系统的 reranker（重排序器）性能提升仅在罪名固定的条件下表现出微弱优势，证明当前的高分主要归功于基准测试的结构性设计。

Conclusion: 中文法律案例检索（LCR）基准存在严重的构念效度问题。目前的NDCG@10指标很大程度上是由“罪名一致性”这一强特征驱动的，而非模型习得了真正的法律推理能力。研究提出了电荷控制评估协议（CCE），呼吁在评估未来基准测试时进行严格的筛选。

Abstract: Chinese Legal Case Retrieval (LCR) benchmarks grade a reference judgment relevant when its legal characterization matches the query, and strong systems now reach NDCG@10 of 0.85-0.88. Most of the BM25-to-best-trained gap is recoverable with no retrieval model: ranking candidates only by shared primary charge, broken by BM25, closes 99.2% of it on LeCaRDv2 -- with no detectable difference from the best-trained system. This reflects benchmark design: LeCaRDv2 defines top relevance via the crime's key constitutive elements, which encode the charge, so same-charge cases are relevant by construction (relevance lift 4.49; charge-to-relevance macro-AUC 0.871). Holding charge fixed, the trained reranker's advantage over BM25 collapses to a small within-charge residual (+0.026 NDCG@10, cluster-bootstrap CI excluding zero, about a quarter), the only non-definitional positive. The effect is not uniform: the same rule recovers 84.3% on LeCaRDv1 and is out of spec on CAIL2022, with the charge-to-relevance signal weakening in step (macro-AUC 0.871/0.759/0.728); a predicted-charge cascade reproduces 76.6% on LeCaRDv2 but does not transfer. The construct is also cashable at first stage: an exploratory zero-training charge-pool channel lifts LeCaRDv2 recall (R@100 +0.025, wrong-charge controls hurt), reported as a positive control for the confound, not a retrieval method or novelty claim. Charge is thus a high-leverage construct-validity factor at the benchmark level -- not auniform explanation of NDCG@10, and not evidence that any system relies on charge. We package established construct-validity and partial-input checks as a reusable charge-controlled protocol (CCE); on all three benchmarks its triggers come back null or descriptive, behaving as designed. We release the scripts, schema, and protocol so future benchmarks can be screened before their NDCG@10 is read as legal-reasoning ability.

</details>


### [338] [CFALR: Collaborative Filtering-Augmented Large Language Model for Personalized Fashion Outfit Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13001)
*Yujuan Ding,Junrong Liao,Yunshan Ma,Yi Bin,Wenqi Fan,Tat-Seng Chua,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CFALR，这是一个融合协同过滤与大语言模型的新型服装推荐框架，通过学习时尚语义与用户交互，显著提升了服装搭配推荐的准确性与个性化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协同过滤在数据稀疏和建模复杂关系上存在局限，而基于模板的方法缺乏灵活性；研究旨在结合LLM的语义推理能力与CF的交互捕捉能力，实现更精准且个性化的服装搭配推荐。

Method: 1. 将用户-服装交互转化为自然语言描述，利用大语言模型（LLM）提取时尚语义；2. 使用基于协同过滤（CF）增强的嵌入（embeddings）对齐语义空间与协同交互空间；3. 引入可训练的投影层（projection layers）整合关系特征与内容特征；4. 采用CF增强的生成机制来处理复杂的服装组合空间。

Result: 在 Polyvore 和 IQON 数据集上的实验结果表明，CFALR 在“个性化填空（fill-in-the-blank）”和“个性化服装生成”任务中均取得了优于传统CF模型和现有LLM基准模型的效果。

Conclusion: CFALR 框架通过融合协同过滤的交互信息与大语言模型的语义理解能力，有效解决了服装推荐中的数据稀疏性及复杂组合匹配难题，并在多项基准测试中展现出优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Personalized outfit recommendation poses a significant challenge in e-commerce and social media platforms, requiring systems that balance user preferences with aesthetic compatibility. Collaborative filtering (CF) provides a traditional solution for this, but it struggles with data-sparse scenarios and complex user-item-outfit relationships. Meanwhile, existing template-based approaches are constrained by rigid pre-designed structures. To bridge these research gaps, we introduce CFALR (Collaborative Filtering-Augmented Large Language Model for Recommendation), a novel framework that synergizes collaborative filtering with large language models for personalized outfit recommendation. Specifically, CFALR describes user-outfit interactions in natural language and leverages LLMs to capture fashion semantics while employing CF-enhanced embeddings to bridge the semantic space and the collaborative interaction spaces. Our technical contributions include: (1) the first LLM-based architecture specifically designed for personalized outfit recommendation, (2) a CF-augmented generative mechanism that efficiently navigates the extensive combination space of outfit items, and (3) trainable projection layers that optimally integrate relational and content features. Experiments on Polyvore and IQON benchmarks demonstrate CFALR's superior performance over both traditional CF-based and LLM-based methods in personalized fill-in-the-blank and personalized outfit generation tasks.

</details>


### [339] [The Clustering Strikes Back: Building Cost-Effective and High-Performance ANNS at Scale with Helmsman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13145)
*Yuchen Huang,Baiteng Ma,Yiping Sun,Yang Shi,Xiao Chen,Xiaocheng Zhong,Zhiyong Wang,Yao Hu,Erci Xu,Chuliang Weng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大规模 ANN 搜索内存成本高昂的问题，小红书提出 HELMSMAN 系统。通过自研用户态存储栈、学习型动态剪枝及 GPU 构建加速，在全闪存服务器上实现了极高的性价比，以极少的资源支撑了超大规模搜索负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小红书现有的基于内存的 HNSW 图索引方案在处理亿级数据时，内存开销过大（0.35 PB DRAM）导致极高的运维成本（CapEx/OpEx），且传统磁盘及内核 I/O 栈性能无法满足高吞吐低延迟的 SLA 要求。

Method: 提出 HELMSMAN 系统，包含：1. 面向 ANNS 特性优化的用户态存储栈（User-space Storage Stack）；2. 基于学习的动态剪枝模块（Leveling-learned Pruning）；3. GPU 加速的索引构建流水线（GPU-accelerated Construction Pipeline）。

Result: 在生产环境中，HELMSMAN 实现了超过 90% 的硬件成本节省；将原先需要 35,000 核及 0.35 PB 内存的负载缩减至 40 台服务器，并支持亿级规模索引在数小时内完成构建。

Conclusion: HELMSMAN 通过软硬件协同设计显著提升了 ANNS 的性能与成本效率，证明了基于全闪存存储的聚类索引方案在超大规模计算场景下具备替代内存图索引的可行性。

Abstract: RedNote (a.k.a., Xiaohongshu, a global-scale social network platform) widely adopts approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) to power its search, recommendation, and advertising services. Due to the demanding Service Level Agreements (SLAs), we have to rely on in-memory graph-based ANNS (i.e., HNSW) to provide high throughput and low latency.
  However, the ever-growing user base and content volume have led to an explosive increase in memory footprint and consequently huge CapEx and OpEx. After exploring various alternatives, we find that building a clustering-based ANNS on top of all-flash servers can be promising. Yet, we still experience severe overheads from the kernel I/O stack, a fixed pruning strategy, and slow index construction.
  We present HELMSMAN, a high-performance and cost-effective clustering-based ANNS system, which combines an ANNS-oriented userspace storage stack, a leveling-learned pruning module, and GPU-accelerated pipelines of construction. HELMSMAN saves over 90% of hardware costs and enables billion-scale index (re)builds within hours. In the current production deployment, operating stably for several months, 40 machines now host ANNS workloads that previously required about 35,000 cores and 0.35 PB DRAM.

</details>


### [340] [CoDeR: Local Constraint-Compatible Retrieval Beyond Semantic Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13204)
*Xingkun Yin,Xuebin Tang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对检索系统处理负面约束查询失效的问题，本文提出CoDeR框架，通过引入兼容性评分器分离约束与主题信号，实现高效、无需LLM的约束敏感检索，显著提升了检索结果的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信息检索系统过度依赖语义相似度，但在处理包含负面约束（如排除属性、否定关系）的查询时，会错误地将符合主题但违反约束条件的文档排在前面，导致“约束违规证据暴露”。

Method: 1. 架构：由一个标准主题编码器（保证覆盖范围）和一个基于对比学习的兼容性评分器（Bi-encoder）组成。 2. 训练：采用针对“满足条件”与“违反条件”证据的词汇极性监督进行对比学习。 3. 推理：将兼容性信号用于重排序或辅助候选集融合，无需引入大语言模型（LLM）。

Result: 在反义词、否定和排除三个受控诊断集上，CoDeR较最强基线将V@2指标分别降低了20.59、23.53和5.77点，并显著优化了FVR（First Violating Rank）指标。

Conclusion: CoDeR通过分离主题相关性和约束兼容性，有效解决了传统密集检索系统在处理负面约束查询时的“约束违规证据暴露”问题，且无需LLM即可显著提升检索准确率。

Abstract: Information retrieval systems have long treated semantic similarity as a proxy for relevance. For constraint-sensitive queries, this proxy can fail when a document is topically close to the query but supports the opposite constraint direction, such as satisfying an attribute that should be excluded or affirming a relation that should be negated. We study this failure as constraint-violating evidence exposure and propose CoDeR, a local constraint-compatible dense retrieval method that separates topical relevance from constraint compatibility. CoDeR keeps a standard topical encoder for candidate coverage and adds a compatibility scorer, implemented as a bi-encoder, trained with lexical-polarity supervision over contrastive satisfying and violating evidences. The compatibility signal can be used to rescore topical candidates or to retrieve an auxiliary compatibility-oriented candidate set, producing a ranked document list without external Large Language Model~(LLM) calls at inference time. We evaluate CoDeR on controlled diagnostics and public negative-constraint retrieval benchmarks. Across three controlled diagnostic sets targeting antonymy, negation, and exclusion, CoDeR reduces V@2 by 20.59, 23.53, and 5.77 points relative to the strongest non-CoDeR baselines, and improves FVR by pushing the first violating document deeper in the ranking.

</details>


### [341] [CQC-RAG: Robust Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Cross-Query Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13438)
*Yanjia Sun,Sifan Liu,Jie Shao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CQC-RAG 框架，通过引入“跨查询一致性”评估机制，在无需额外监督的情况下，利用多角度的语义等价查询来验证回答的置信度稳定性，有效减少了 RAG 模型中因检索噪声导致的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 方法在应对语义等价但句法不同的查询时，检索结果差异较大，且容易受无关/误导性文档的影响产生幻觉。现有的多路径推理方法存在多样性注入不可控及答案评估视角单一的局限性。

Method: 提出“跨查询一致性假设”（Cross-Query Consistency Hypothesis），设计了 CQC-RAG 框架。其核心步骤包括：1. 将原问题改写为语义等价但句法多样的查询；2. 对共享文档池进行重排序以构建查询受限的推理语境；3. 采用证据导向协议提取答案-证据对；4. 根据跨多查询语境的置信度稳定性选择最终答案。

Result: 在四个开放域问答数据集上的实验表明，CQC-RAG 显著优于现有的多查询基准方法，在 TriviaQA 上 EM 值提升了 4.76 个百分点，在 MuSiQue 上提升了 9.12 个百分点。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 CQC-RAG 框架通过利用跨查询一致性假设，能够有效识别并过滤噪声诱导的幻觉，提升了 RAG 系统的鲁棒性，无需外部监督及额外的检索覆盖扩展。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a common approach for improving the factuality of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet its reliability remains highly sensitive to how external evidence is retrieved and used. Semantically equivalent queries with different syntactic forms may lead to different retrieval results, while irrelevant or misleading documents can further induce hallucinated answers. Existing multi-path reasoning methods improve robustness by sampling multiple candidate answers and applying voting- or confidence-based selection, but they still face two limitations: diversity is often injected through uncontrollable decoding randomness, and answer evaluation is usually confined to a single query-induced evidence view. To address these limitations, we propose a Cross-Query Consistency Hypothesis: correct answers tend to maintain high confidence across semantically equivalent but syntactically diverse queries, whereas noise-induced hallucinations exhibit unstable confidence under such query variations. Based on this hypothesis, we introduce CQC-RAG, a framework that co-designs query-level diversity injection with cross-query consistency evaluation. CQC-RAG rewrites the original question into diverse but meaning-preserving queries, reranks a shared document pool to construct query-conditioned reasoning contexts, applies an evidence-grounded protocol to extract answer-evidence pairs and selects answers according to their confidence stability across these contexts. This design enables self-evaluation without external supervision and does not rely on expanded retrieval coverage. Experiments on four open-domain question answering benchmarks show that CQC-RAG outperforms the strongest previous multi-query baseline by +4.76 pp EM on TriviaQA and +9.12 pp EM on MuSiQue, validating the effectiveness of cross-query consistency for filtering noise-induced hallucinations.

</details>


### [342] [OneRetrieval: Unifying Multi-Branch E-commerce Retrieval with an Editable Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13533)
*Xuxin Zhang,Ben Chen,Yue Lv,Siyuan Wang,Yupeng Li,Yufei Ma,Zihan Liang,Tong Zhao,Ying Yang,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao,Zhipeng Qian,Xinyu Sun,Chenyi Lei,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OneRetrieval 是一种具备“实时可编辑性”的工业级生成式检索模型，通过 KAE 机制解决了生成式模型无法处理突发新词项的痛点，成功在快手大规模搜索场景落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级电商搜索引擎的传统多路召回存在维护成本高、缺乏联合优化的问题；现有的生成式检索（GR）模型要么无法实现实时的新词插入（闭集限制），要么依赖模型的泛化能力（开放词表限制），缺乏工业生产所需的实时可编辑能力。

Method: 提出 OneRetrieval 框架，采用关键词对齐编码（KAE）将标识符位置与属性词绑定；利用信息论合并方法组织 18 个属性类别；设计预留槽位机制支持无重训练的实时词项插入；通过四阶段微调流水线优化质量与可编辑性。

Result: 在五百万次真实流量请求中，OneRetrieval 的召回质量与最强 GR 基线持平，干预命中率比闭集方法高一个数量级；在快手（Kuaishou）上线后，提升了订单转化率，且在替代全量召回分支时保持了高点击率（CTR），现已承载每日亿级的页面浏览量。

Conclusion: OneRetrieval 实现了生成式检索（GR）在工业场景下的落地，证明了通过关键词对齐编码（KAE）机制，可以在保证检索性能的同时实现模型实时可编辑性，成功替代了复杂的传统多路召回架构。

Abstract: Industrial e-commerce search serves hundreds of millions of items through a multi-branch retrieval stage fused by hand-tuned merging without joint optimization. Generative retrieval (GR) raises the prospect of collapsing this stage into a single model, yet unification is gated by more than retrieval quality: the inverted-index branch converts below the platform average yet persists because it is almost the only branch where operations can inject a new term within hours without any model update; a one-model substitute must preserve this real-time editability. Existing GR methods structurally lack it: closed-codebook methods fix each slot to a quantized embedding at training, while open-vocabulary methods leave new-term routing to model generalization. We present OneRetrieval, a one-model GR framework built on Keyword-Aligned Encoding (KAE), which ties each identifier position to an interpretable attribute word, pairing competitive recall quality with the editability of the inverted index -- to our knowledge the first editable generative retrieval method. An information-theoretic merging organizes 18 attribute categories into six codebook groups with non-uniform capacity; reserved slots in each codebook can be bound to new words after deployment without retraining; and a four-stage fine-tuning pipeline secures quality and editability jointly. On five million real-traffic requests, OneRetrieval matches the deep recall of the strongest generative baseline, with an intervention hit rate over an order of magnitude above closed-codebook encodings. Online, replacing the inverted-index branch significantly lifts order volume; extending to nearly the entire stage holds conversion while improving CTR. The system is deployed at Kuaishou, serving hundreds of millions of PVs daily.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [343] [ITME: Inference Tiered Memory Expansion with Disaggregated CXL-Hybrid Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12556)
*Hakbeom Jang,Younghoon Min,Sunwoong Kim,Taeyoung Ahn,Hanyee Kim,Youngpyo Joo,Hoshik Kim,Jongryool Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ITME通过CXL混合内存技术实现TB级远程内存扩展，优化了超长上下文LLM推理中的KV缓存管理，显著提升了系统吞吐量并降低了复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM向智能体和超长上下文发展，单机存储已无法满足TB级上下文状态需求。现有的基于DPU和NVMe-oF的共享存储方案在软件优化和成本效率方面仍面临挑战，亟需更高效的共享上下文架构。

Method: 提出了一种名为ITME（Inference Tiered Memory Expansion）的系统，利用CXL混合内存（结合CMM和NVMe SSD）提供TB级字节可寻址远程内存扩展，并通过利用模型权重和前缀缓存的确定性访问模式，主动管理内存-存储层级之间的数据移动。

Result: 通过使用SK Hynix CMM和PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSD以及FPGA硬件原型验证，ITME通过扩展主机内存限制下的KV缓存足迹，实现了高达35.7%的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: ITME通过利用CXL混合内存架构，实现了TB级字节可寻址远程内存扩展，有效解决了LLM推理中大规模KV缓存的存储瓶颈，在成本效率和系统吞吐量方面均有显著提升。

Abstract: The rapid shift toward agentic and long-context workloads in Large Language Models (LLMs) is pushing the industry beyond the capacity of individual servers toward disaggregated shared storage to handle TB-scale context states. This movement has led to the emergence of specialized shared context layers designed to externalize and share cumulative inference states across distributed clusters. While offloading to a data processing unit (DPU) within just-a-bunch-of-flash (JBOF) architectures accelerates NVMe-over-fabrics (NVMe-oF) target processing, the need for sophisticated software-level optimization and cost-efficiency burdens remain significant. Consequently, the ideal architecture for scaling this shared context infrastructure is still an active area of exploration. In this paper, we propose ITME (Inference Tiered Memory Expansion), which leverages a CXL-hybrid memory to present a massive, TB-scale byte-addressable remote memory expansion. This approach enables cost-efficient scaling and simplifies the software stack through direct byte-addressability, effectively addressing the challenges of shared context infrastructure. Our key insight is that the deterministic access patterns of voluminous model weights and prefix caches enable the system to proactively manage data movement across the memory-storage hierarchy. We validate ITME by evaluating its performance potential with production-grade SK Hynix CMM and PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSDs, while further demonstrating its functional feasibility through an FPGA-based hardware prototype. Overall, ITME enhances conventional CPU-offloading by providing additional remote memory expansion to accommodate large KV cache footprints beyond host memory limits, achieving up to a 35.7\% throughput improvement.

</details>


### [344] [GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13501)
*Xinwei Qiang,Yifan Hu,Shixuan Sun,Jing Yang,Han Zhao,Chen Chen,Yu Feng,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GF-DiT 是一个针对扩散模型推理的弹性调度运行时，通过将 GPU 并行度转变为动态可调度资源，并配合异步任务分解与极低开销的动态重构技术，显著优化了推理系统的吞吐量与响应延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型（DiT）推理系统采用静态并行配置，无法适应请求、执行阶段及系统环境的多样性与异构性，导致 GPU 利用率低、延迟高及服务等级协议（SLO）违规率高。

Method: 1. 提出异步执行抽象：将扩散模型推理请求分解为独立的轨迹任务（trajectory tasks），实现请求生命周期内的动态资源重分配； 2. 引入免分组通信（Group-free Collectives）：一种轻量级通信范式，允许低开销的在线通信组重构。

Result: 在 vLLM-Omni 中实现并测试显示：吞吐量提升至 6.01 倍，平均延迟降低 95%，SLO 违规率降低 90%，通信组建立开销从 778 毫秒降至约 60 微秒。

Conclusion: GF-DiT 通过引入异步执行抽象和免分组通信范式，成功解决了扩散模型推理中静态并行配置导致的资源利用率低和调度效率差的问题，证明了将 GPU 并行度作为可调度资源在弹性推理系统中的可行性与显著优势。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality.
  This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups.
  We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.

</details>


### [345] [Eidola: Modeling Multi-GPU Network Communication Traffic in Distributed AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12638)
*Ranganath R. Selagamsetty,Matthew Poremba,Bradford M. Beckmann,Joshua San Miguel,Mikko H. Lipasti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Eidola 是一个基于 gem5 的可扩展 GPU 通信模拟器，通过特征提取与时序标注，实现了对大规模多 GPU 环境下不规则流量和同步行为的精确建模与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具难以对大规模分布式 AI 训练中核融合和重叠通信带来的不规则、瞬态流量进行精确建模，亟需一个既能保持扩展性又具备周期级精度的模拟框架来分析互连带宽与延迟压力。

Method: 在 gem5 模拟框架基础上构建，通过“eidolon”（幻影）模型提取 GPU 应用的最小化特征，利用标注过的真实应用时序配置文件（timing profiles），以周期级精度模拟点对点（P2P）GPU 写入流量。

Result: 成功复现了融合核执行中的变异性，并验证了 SyncMon 架构机制在减少轮询相关内存流量方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Eidola 是一个灵活且可扩展的模拟平台，能够精确建模大规模分布式 GPU 系统中的互连通信行为，支持架构研究与性能优化。

Abstract: As distributed AI workloads grow in scale, multi-GPU systems have become essential for training large models. Although techniques like kernel fusion and overlapping communication with computation help reduce delays, they also introduce irregular and transient traffic patterns that are difficult to model using existing tools. These techniques rely heavily on fine-grained synchronization and peer-to-peer communication, which place significant pressure on interconnect bandwidth and latency.
  In this work, we introduce Eidola, a scalable extension to the gem5 simulation framework that enables detailed modeling of inter-GPU communication traffic. The extension is scalable as our GPU model serves as a succinct eidolon, emulating the minimal characteristics needed for traffic modeling. Eidola uses annotated timing profiles from real applications to emulate peer-to-peer GPU writes with cycle-level precision. This allows researchers to simulate and analyze synchronization behavior across large multi-GPU configurations. The simulator supports configurable per-GPU traffic patterns and enables isolated performance analysis under different communication scenarios.
  We demonstrate Eidola's effectiveness by reproducing variability in fused kernel execution and by implementing a SyncMon-inspired synchronization mechanism, confirming reductions in polling-related memory traffic. Our results show that Eidola provides a flexible and scalable platform for studying inter-GPU communication and supports architectural exploration in modern distributed GPU systems.

</details>


### [346] [On the Limits of Performance Portability in Directive-Based GPU Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12753)
*Alessandro Romeo,Nitin Shukla,Stefano Truzzi,Alessio Suriano,Andrea Mignone*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究评估了 gPLUTO 代码在不同 GPU 架构下的性能可移植性，揭示了当前基于指令的编程模型在跨 AMD/NVIDIA 硬件时面临严重的性能差距，主要由内存访问模式、编译优化限制及抽象层负载引起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用向 GPU 加速的百亿亿次（exascale）系统迁移时，在性能、可移植性和生产力之间面临权衡。该研究旨在评估基于指令（directive-based）的 GPU 编程在不同架构间的性能可移植性。

Method: 将生产级磁流体动力学代码 gPLUTO 从 OpenACC 移植到 OpenMP，并在 NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) 和 AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) 设备上进行性能评估与剖析，重点分析内核级性能瓶颈及编译器差异。

Result: NVIDIA 架构上 OpenACC 与 OpenMP 性能相当；在 AMD MI250X 上，OpenMP 版本应用级性能比 NVIDIA OpenACC 基准慢约 3 倍，内核级性能降幅高达一个数量级。瓶颈主要在于对步长内存访问模式的敏感度、编译器局限性，以及低并行内核中 C++ 抽象层导致的寄存器压力和溢出。

Conclusion: 实现跨 GPU 架构的性能可移植性不仅需要应用程序级的调整，还依赖于编译器后端的持续改进以及针对特定体系结构的优化策略。

Abstract: The transition of scientific applications to GPU-accelerated exascale systems is constrained by trade-offs between performance, portability, and productivity. This work evaluates the performance portability of directive-based GPU programming by porting gPLUTO, a production-grade magnetohydrodynamics code for astrophysical simulations, from OpenACC to OpenMP, and analyzing its performance on NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) and AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) devices. On NVIDIA platforms, OpenACC and OpenMP achieve comparable performance due to a shared compiler backend, providing a consistent baseline for assessing algorithmic efficiency. In contrast, the same OpenMP implementation is approximately three times slower at the application level on AMD MI250X with respect to the NVIDIA A100 OpenACC baseline, with kernel-level slowdowns reaching up to an order of magnitude, driven by sensitivity to strided memory-access patterns and compiler limitations. Kernel-level profiling shows that the dominant contributors to run-time are memory-latency-bound rather than limited by peak band-width. In low-parallelism kernels, C++ abstraction layers increase register pressure and spilling, leading to extreme slowdowns of up to 47x in specific cases. These results indicate that portable performance across GPU architectures requires not only application-level changes but also continued advances in compiler backends and architecture-aware optimization strategies

</details>


### [347] [SemanticXR: Low Power and Real-time Queryable Semantic Mapping with an Object-Level Device-Cloud Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12849)
*Rahul Singh,Devdeep Ray,Connor Smith,Sarita Adve*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SemanticXR 是首个面向 XR 的设备-云协作系统，通过以“对象”为中心的管理策略，在满足严格的低功耗、低带宽和有限资源限制下，实现了高效、实时且具备扩展性的开放词汇表语义映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义映射方案计算密集且依赖服务器级高性能资源，难以在移动端 XR 设备上实现实时的开放词汇映射；现有的云卸载方案缺乏针对 XR 环境（功率、带宽、内存限制）的专门优化（如拆分策略、资源管理）。

Method: 提出了一种设备-云协同的系统框架，核心机制包括：1) 将可识别对象提升为跨设备的通信、计算和内存管理基本单位；2) 服务器端采用对象级并行处理和几何降采样以降低延迟，并结合深度图协同设计以减少上行带宽；3) 设备端维护对象级稀疏局部图，实现增量更新和优先级调度以保障查询稳健性；4) 提供可配置的资源与精度权衡机制以适应不同应用需求。

Result: 相较于对比基准，SemanticXR 将服务器端映射延迟降低了 2.2 倍；上行带宽保持在 2.5 Mbps 以下；在处理多达 10,000 个物体时，查询延迟低于 100 ms；且能在 500 MB 内存限制下支持数万个物体，同时将设备额外功耗控制在 2% 以内。

Conclusion: SemanticXR 通过将语义对象作为系统管理的核心单元（对象级协同），成功解决了现实延伸（XR）设备在语义映射中面临的性能瓶颈。该系统证明了在低功耗、有限带宽和有限内存的设备上实现高性能、开放词汇表的实时语义映射是可行的，并为移动端空间计算提供了实用的部署方案。

Abstract: Semantic mapping is a core service that enables grounded interactions in emerging Extended Reality (XR) applications such as AI assistants and spatial object search. Deploying this capability on mobile XR devices requires a system that is open-vocabulary, real-time, and low-power. Existing approaches are compute-intensive and assume server-class resources. Cloud offloading offers a practical path, but no existing system splits semantic mapping across the device-cloud boundary or manages its communication, execution, and memory footprint.
  We present SemanticXR, the first device-cloud system for real-time, open-vocabulary semantic mapping and querying under XR power, bandwidth, and memory constraints. Our key insight is to elevate semantically identifiable objects to first-class units of communication, execution, and memory across the device and server. On the server, object-level parallelism and geometry downsampling improve mapping latency, while object-level depth-mapping co-design reduces upstream bandwidth. On the device, an object-level sparse local map with incremental updates and update prioritization enables network-robust querying with bounded memory and downstream bandwidth. Object-level configurable resource usage vs. quality trade-offs let applications and the system adapt mapping to application requirements and operating conditions, respectively.
  Against a device-cloud baseline with the same perception models, object-level organization improves server-side mapping latency by 2.2X at equal semantic quality. Depth-mapping co-design maintains upstream bandwidth under 2.5 Mbps. On the device, SemanticXR sustains sub-100 ms query latency for up to 10,000 objects even under network drops, supports tens of thousands of objects within 500 MB, and scales downstream bandwidth with map changes, not total scene size. The system adds only 2% device power during normal operation.

</details>


### [348] [High-Order Spectral Element Methods for Wave Propagation on ARM Multicore CPU with SME: Optimizations and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12850)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Wubing Wan,Zekun Yin,Wenqiang Wang,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对ARM架构的SME扩展优化了SPECFEM3D应用，通过核心计算核优化与并行策略改进，在提升4-6倍性能的同时，推动了SEM算法在离散化方法上向更高阶多项式迁移，从而减少计算时间和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的谱元法（SEM）实现无法充分利用配备可扩展矩阵扩展（SME）技术的新兴ARM多核CPU的硬件潜能。

Method: 1. 针对SME指令集设计了批处理小矩阵核心计算核；2. 针对有限高带宽内存（HBM）系统采用了内存感知型混合MPI+OpenMP执行策略；3. 基于色散关系进行了(h,p)权衡的等精度分析。

Result: 在固定多项式阶数下，优化后的实现相比原代码性能提升了4至6倍，且显著优于未启用SME的基准CPU性能；此外，SME的应用使得在等精度前沿下，系统倾向于选择更高的多项式阶数。

Conclusion: SME不仅提升了计算核的效率，还改变了谱元法（SEM）在现代ARM多核平台上的离散化策略，使得更高阶的多项式逼近在性能上变得更具经济性。

Abstract: Wave propagation based on the spectral element method (SEM) is a representative HPC workload, but existing SEM implementations are not well matched to emerging ARM multicore CPUs with Scalable Matrix Extension (SME). We present an SME-enabled optimization of \textsc{SPECFEM3D} on the emerging LX2 processor that combines an SME-aware batched small-matrix kernel for SEM tensor-product operators, a memory-aware hybrid MPI+OpenMP execution scheme for limited-HBM systems, and a dispersion-based iso-accuracy study of the $(h,p)$ tradeoff. At fixed polynomial order, the optimized implementation improves full-application performance by 4--6$\times$ over the original code and delivers clear gains over optimized non-SME CPU baselines. Beyond these implementation-level gains, our results suggest that SME shifts the performance-favorable operating point toward higher polynomial orders along the dispersion-based iso-accuracy frontier, further reducing time-to-solution and working-set size. These results indicate that SME affects not only kernel efficiency, but also the practical discretization tradeoff for SEM on modern ARM multicore platforms.

</details>


### [349] [Maestro: Workload-Aware Cross-Cluster Scheduling for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12950)
*Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Yihui Zhang,Yilong Li,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Maestro 是一个工作负载感知的 LLM 多智能体调度系统，通过智能的资源预测和分层调度策略解决内存碎片化与长尾延迟问题，在有限 GPU 预算下大幅提升了系统执行效率和 SLO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）在复杂任务处理中表现出色，但其调用过程产生的高额非确定性资源消耗、内存碎片化及多模型部署带来的调度难题，在资源受限的环境下难以高效运行。

Method: Maestro 提出了一种工作负载感知的调度系统：1. 通过预测各阶段输出长度和内存占用指导调度；2. 节点级实现动态多模型共存（通过分层权重缓存和弹性内存预配）；3. 集群级执行延迟感知路由以降低冷启动影响；4. 全局级采用工作流感知优先级排序。

Result: 在原型实验和追踪驱动的模拟中，Maestro 将 KV 预留 HBM 降低了 67.2%，在高竞争场景下的 SLO 达成率较 EDF（最早截止时间优先算法）提升了 23.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: Maestro 通过利用多智能体系统的语义信息和分层调度策略，有效解决了大语言模型多智能体系统在资源受限环境下的部署挑战，显著降低了内存消耗并提升了服务级别目标（SLO）的达成率。

Abstract: Large Language Model based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for tackling complex tasks by breaking them into collaborative workflows of specialized LLM-powered agents. However, deploying such multi-agent workloads at scale poses significant system challenges. Each user query spawns an iterative pipeline of LLM calls, greatly amplifying resource consumption compared to single-turn queries. In resource-constrained cloud settings, these workflows face non-deterministic and input-dependent costs at decode stage, heavy-tailed multi-model requirements with memory fragmentation and over-provisioning, and cross-cluster scheduling trade-offs. We present Maestro, a workload-aware scheduling system designed for LLM-MAS serving under strict GPU budgets. Maestro explicitly leverages agent semantics and roles: it predicts the output length and memory usage of each stage and uses this prediction to drive a hierarchical scheduler. At the node level, Maestro enables dynamic multi-model co-location via hierarchical weight caching and elastic memory provisioning. At the cluster level, it performs latency-aware routing to avoid cold-start delays and memory overloads. At the global level, it enforces workflow-aware prioritization to minimize head-of-line blocking for interactive tasks. Across prototype experiments and trace-driven simulations, Maestro reduces KV-reservation HBM by 67.2% and improves high-contention SLO attainment over EDF by 23.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [350] [JiRAIYA: A Reputation-Based Hierarchical Federated Learning Framework on Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13180)
*Venkata Raghava Kurada,Pallav Kumar Baruah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Web3技术的联邦学习框架，通过分层共识、新颖性检测及声誉机制，解决了传统联邦学习中透明度不足和审计困难的问题，并有效防御了恶意攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的联邦学习系统在企业环境部署时，由于缺乏透明度和审计能力，导致聚合过程不透明（难以判断模型更新被采纳或丢弃的原因），且现有的缓解策略往往带来过高的计算和通信开销。

Method: 1. 采用分层架构，引入委托管理器（Delegated Managers）进行编排；2. 结合新颖性检测（Novelty Detection）与共识机制（Consensus Mechanisms）来防御对抗性攻击和中毒攻击；3. 利用Web3技术对模型更新进行编码与全网广播，由管理器共同验证；4. 引入基于声誉评分（Reputation Score）的备份机制以保证模型生成。

Result: 通过真实场景实验验证，该框架在对抗攻击下表现出色的鲁棒性和高透明度，证明了其在超越传统企业设置下应用联邦学习的潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过融合Web3技术，有效地解决了企业级联邦学习中缺乏透明度和审计能力的问题，在保证模型训练鲁棒性的同时，为去中心化环境下的联邦学习提供了一种可信、透明的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning(FL) is predominantly deployed in enterprise environments, where limited transparency and restricted auditability hinder broader adoption. Existing FL systems often suffer from opaque aggregation processes, making it unclear which model updates are accepted or discarded. Current mitigation strategies typically rely on external validators introducing additional computational and communication overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel FL framework that leverages existing Web3 technologies to enhance transparency, trust and auditability throughout the training process. The framework adopts a hierarchical architecture in which delegated managers orchestrate the FL training process within their respective federations. To mitigate adversarial and poisoning attacks, a combination of novelty detection and consensus mechanisms were employed. Model updates are encoded and broad casted to all managers, who independently evaluate their validity and those model updates that are approved by the consensus are incorporated into the global model. Additionally, a reputation score based backup mechanism is employed to ensure model generation. Extensive experiments conducted under real world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness, resilience of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential to enable transparent FL beyond traditional enterprise setting.

</details>


### [351] [Dynamic Resource Management in Production HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13266)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Guillaume Houzeaux,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为DMR的实用化MPI可塑性架构，能够无缝集成到现有HPC生产环境，在无需修改调度器或应用代码的前提下，实现了在不损失性能的情况下降低能耗与节点资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算应用通常具有随时间变化的负载特性，但现有的HPC集群主要依赖固定规模的资源分配，而现有的动态资源调整技术大多停留在实验原型阶段，难以直接应用于生产环境。

Method: 开发了动态资源管理（DMR）框架，通过提供API接口与现有的HPC软件栈集成。该方法采用非侵入式设计，通过运行时调整MPI进程数量来应对负载的时变性，无需修改应用源码或底层资源调度器。

Result: 在三台TOP500超级计算机的真实生产环境配置下，对两款大型科学应用进行了评估。实验表明，该方案在性能上与静态资源分配基准持平，同时大幅降低了相同工作负载下的节点小时（node-hour）消耗。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，通过DMR框架可以在生产级HPC系统中有效实现MPI可塑性（malleability），且无需修改资源调度器或进行侵入式代码重构，从而降低了动态资源管理在高性能计算领域应用的门槛，并显著提升了资源利用率。

Abstract: Many large-scale scientific applications exhibit time-varying behavior, yet production HPC clusters still rely on rigid, fixed-size allocations, and most dynamic techniques remain confined to laboratory prototypes. This work presents a practical MPI malleability methodology that integrates with state-of-the-art high-performance computing (HPC) software stacks and operational practices. The methodology is implemented in the Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) framework and is designed to ease adoption by existing applications without requiring intrusive code changes or scheduler modifications. We evaluate our approach by integrating the DMR API into two large-scale scientific applications and deploying them on three TOP500 supercomputers under realistic production configurations. Our non-invasive malleability solution achieves performance comparable to static baselines in controlled environments while substantially reducing node-hour consumption for identical workloads. These results show that malleability can be effectively exploited on production systems using vanilla resource managers, lowering the barrier to adoption of dynamic resource management in HPC.

</details>


### [352] [Temporal Conductance and Bounds on the Voter Model for Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13374)
*Tatiana Rocha Avila,Holger Dell,John Lapinskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对动态网络提出了一种新型连通性度量——“时间电导”（temporal conductance），并证明了选民模型在动态图上的期望一致性时间受限于此指标，且该界是理论最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有的静态电导（conductance）度量方法在动态网络中存在局限性（即当某时刻快照断开时电导即崩溃为0），无法有效刻画跨时间维度的连通性对一致性形成的影响。因此，需要一种能更好处理时变网络特性的连通性度量方法。

Method: 1. 提出“时间电导”（temporal conductance）$Φ$ 作为量化动态网络连通性的新指标；2. 理论分析选民模型（voter model）在动态网络上的演化过程；3. 通过数学证明建立了一致性时间与时间电导之间的关系，并利用构造性证明论证了该上界的紧致性（tightness）。

Result: 研究表明，标准选民模型在动态网络中达到一致性的期望时间至多为 $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$，且该 bound 在常数因子范围内是紧致的。该结果推广了 Berenbrink 等人在静态网络上的研究结论。

Conclusion: 本文引入的“时间电导”（temporal conductance）是分析动态网络中随机过程的一种普适性度量。作者证明了基于时间电导的一致性时间上界 $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$ 是紧致的，该指标有望成为分析时变网络动力学及时间非齐次马尔可夫链的重要工具。

Abstract: The voter model is a classical stochastic process that models how opinions might spread through a network: at each step, every node lazily adopts the opinion of a random neighbour; eventually all nodes share the same opinion (consensus). Stronger connectivity should yield faster consensus. Berenbrink, Giakkoupis, Kermarrec, and Mallmann-Trenn (ICALP 2016) make this precise via the network's conductance: if the network has $m$ edges, minimum degree $d_{\min}$, and conductance at least $φ$, then the voter model reaches consensus in expected $O(m/(d_{\min}φ))$ steps. Their results extend to dynamic networks with fixed vertex degrees by considering the network's conductance at each time step.
  We introduce temporal conductance $Φ$, a more general connectivity measure for dynamic networks. Unlike static conductance, which collapses to $0$ whenever some snapshot is disconnected, $Φ$ captures connectivity through edges that appear at different times. We generalise the results of Berenbrink et al. from static conductance to temporal conductance, showing that the expected consensus time of the standard voter model is at most $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is tight up to constant factors. We expect temporal conductance to be a useful primitive for analysing other dynamics on temporal networks, and potentially time-inhomogeneous Markov chains more generally.

</details>


### [353] [Skiplists with Foresight: Skipping Cache Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13321)
*Tomer Cory,Niv Sulimany,Erez Petrank*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Foresight 是一种提升跳表缓存友好性的优化方法，在保证易于集成的同时，显著增强了跳表在单机和高并发数据库场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳表作为一种基础数据结构被广泛用于索引存储，但在缓存效率方面仍有提升空间，尤其是在并发场景下实现高性能存在挑战。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Foresight 的缓存友好型跳表优化方案，并针对并发环境下的同步挑战制定了相应的解决方案。

Result: 在微基准测试中，Foresight 使跳表的吞吐量提升高达 45%；在 DBx1000 内存数据库中，其端到端性能提升高达 15%。

Conclusion: Foresight 是一项简单易用的外科手术式优化技术，能够显著提升跳表在单线程和多线程环境下的性能，且易于集成到现有系统中。

Abstract: A skiplist is a fundamental data structure widely used in systems and applications for indexing data stores. In this work, we introduce Foresight, a cache-friendly skiplist optimization. Extending Foresight to concurrent settings introduces significant synchronization challenges that we identify and address. Foresight is a surgical optimization, easy to integrate into a wide variety of skiplist designs. We apply it to one sequential and three concurrent skiplist designs and observe throughput improvements of up to 45% in microbenchmarks. When applied to a skiplist-based index in the DBx1000 in-memory database, Foresight yields end-to-end performance gains of up to 15%.

</details>


### [354] [Work Stealing for the 2D-Mesh Topology of Satellite Constellations in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13329)
*Mia Reitz,Dorian Chenet,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对空间卫星网状拓扑网络，论文提出了一种仅向直接相连邻居窃取任务的策略，在几乎不损失负载均衡效率的前提下，有效解决了跨多跳高延迟带来的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步多任务（AMT）运行环境大多假设全局低延迟连接，而空间边缘计算（SEC）卫星星座具有稀疏网状拓扑和高路径延迟的特点，这种差异限制了传统全局任务窃取策略的效率。

Method: 提出了一种“仅邻居（neighbor-only）”任务窃取策略；通过建立分析模型对比延迟优势；在HPC集群上通过仿真网状拓扑进行对比实验。

Result: 分析模型显示，该策略的单次窃取延迟优势随网络规模增大而增加；HPC实验证明，在负载平衡和不平衡场景下，该策略性能与全局窃取策略差距仅在2.2%以内。

Conclusion:  neighbor-only work stealing 是一种有效的调度策略，在保证负载均衡性能的同时，显著降低了多跳网络环境下的通信开销，极具扩展潜力。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) is a parallel programming model used in High Performance Computing (HPC). An AMT runtime can distribute fine-grained tasks across processing units called workers, through work stealing: when a worker has no tasks left to process, it tries to steal tasks from other workers. Workers are not restricted to a single compute node but can also be distributed across multiple nodes of an HPC cluster. Existing AMT runtimes assume a fully connected network with low, uniform latency and perform global work stealing, selecting another worker at random from all workers in the system.
  Space Edge Computing (SEC) uses constellations of satellites in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) as distributed compute clusters. Unlike HPC clusters, LEO satellites communicate through inter-satellite links that form a sparse mesh topology. Reaching a distant satellite requires multiple hops, each adding latency.
  As a step toward adapting AMT to SEC, this paper proposes a neighbor-only work stealing strategy in which workers steal exclusively from directly connected neighbors, avoiding multi-hop communication. An analytical model shows that restricting stealing this way yields a per-attempt latency advantage that grows with constellation size. Preliminary experiments on an HPC cluster with an emulated mesh over uniform low-latency links isolate the effect of victim selection: the neighbor-only strategy performs within ~2.2% of global stealing on both balanced and irregular workloads, indicating that restricting the victim set does not harm load balancing in this setting. Taken together, the experiments suggest that neighbor-only stealing can be on a par with global stealing, and the model suggests that neighbor-only stealing becomes preferable at scale.

</details>


### [355] [Finding Conservation Laws of Large Dynamical Systems with Tasks and Futures: A Case Study in Utilizing Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13623)
*Rüdiger Nather*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入 `await_delete` 原语打破了 Future 模型的不可变限制，实现了内存的高效复用，并在大规模矩阵求逆任务中验证了其具有良好的并行扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的并行 Future 模型普遍具有值不可变性（value-immutability），限制了高性能线性代数中至关重要的内存复用（in-place memory updates），导致在处理复杂并行负载时效率受限。

Method: 1. 提出 `await_delete` 语义：扩展传统 Future 语义，在消费者完成任务后实现安全的内存重用；2. 应用场景：基于此语义设计了块状对称矩阵求逆算法；3. 实现与实验：在 Taskflow 框架上进行实现，并进行强扩展性（strong-scaling）评估。

Result: 在小型问题规模上 Future 存在显著开销，但在大型矩阵计算中实现了近乎线性的扩展性，并确定了实现 amortized（摊销）高性能的阈值。

Conclusion: 通过引入 await_delete 语义，使得并行任务模型（Futures）能够支持高性能线性代数所需的内存重用，从而验证了 Futures 作为大规模线性代数计算工具的可行性。

Abstract: As parallel workloads grow in complexity, managing fine-grained data dependencies becomes a critical challenge. Futures offer a promising model for handling these dependencies, particularly in irregular algorithms, but they also come with the restriction of value-immutability. This immutability limits the ability to perform in-place memory updates, a necessity for high-performance linear algebra where memory recycling is paramount.
  In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a new construct, await_delete, which extends traditional future semantics to allow safe value reuse once consumers are finished. Building on this extension, we present a novel future-based algorithm for the block-wise inversion of dense, symmetric matrices, motivated by a recent algorithm for finding conservation laws of dynamical systems.
  We implement our approach in an extended version of Taskflow and evaluate it through strong-scaling experiments. Our results demonstrate that while futures incur significant overhead on smaller problem sizes, they achieve nearly linear scaling on large matrices. We analyze the amortization threshold and show that futures are a viable high-performance tool for large-scale linear algebra.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [356] [Non-Parametric Dual-Manifold Mapping via 8-Bit Bounded Transformation Matrices: Challenging FP-centric Hardware Paradigms in Low-Energy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13328)
*Lars Kopp*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于8位整数逻辑的双流形映射框架，通过消除浮点运算和训练需求，实现了极高弹性的边缘AI计算范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习硬件过度依赖浮点算术（FP32/16/8），导致了不可持续的热能和算力开销。

Method: 提出了一种非参数、免训练的“双流形映射（Dual-Manifold Mapping）”框架；使用8位有符号整数边界内的位运算和累加逻辑，通过Z矩阵映射空间和结构流形，利用指针偏移、位掩码以及固定阈值实现推理，并采用局部受限的随机噪声注入机制进行更新。

Result: 系统在90%截断稀疏度和20%节点损坏的情况下，通过全局缩放因子保持了趋近完美的重建能力，且推理过程无需浮点乘法器。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了通过8位整型运算和位运算替代浮点数算力的可行性，并指出神经形态边缘计算是超越传统稠密浮点GPU架构的新路径。

Abstract: Modern deep learning hardware paradigms rely heavily on computationally expensive floating-point arithmetic (FP32, FP16, and FP8), requiring massive thermal and energetic overheads to maintain gradient-based optimization. This paper introduces a non-parametric, training-free computational framework for dual-manifold mapping that operates strictly within an 8-bit signed integer boundary and leverages simple bitwise and accumulation logic. By mapping a Spatial Manifold (N_spatial = 8192 neurons) and a Gabor-pooled Structural Manifold (N_structural = 4096 neurons) through an integer-based transformation matrix (Z-matrix), we eliminate the need for floating-point multipliers. Inference is achieved via cache-friendly pointer offsets and bitwise masks, accumulating directional sign-charges using fixed thresholds (theta_reject = 8.0, theta_cut = 2.0). Learning is executed through a localized, bounded update mechanism restricted strictly within [-127, 127], modulated by stochastic noise injection. Both architectures demonstrate extreme holographic resilience, preserving near-perfect reconstruction via a global scaling factor under 90% truncation sparsity and 20% random node destruction. By reducing core AI inference to 8-bit boundaries and boolean-like execution, this framework outlines a paradigm shift toward neuromorphic edge-computing, directly questioning the long-term necessity of dense, floating-point-centric GPU accelerators.

</details>


### [357] [SupraSNN: Exploiting Synapse-Level Parallelism in Spiking Neural Network Accelerators through Co-Optimized Mapping and Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13354)
*Seyed Sadra Ghavami,Mohammad Hossein Nikkhah,Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SupraSNN是一种受超标量架构启发的硬软件协同设计SNN加速器，通过解耦突触与神经元计算实现硬件大规模并行，显著提升了能效与推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNNs）在大规模并行实现上面临硬件执行效率的挑战；受处理器超标量（Superscalar）架构启发，旨在通过将突触事件视为可并行化的微操作来解决计算效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出SupraSNN架构：1）硬件架构：物理分离突触与神经元计算，采用多播树分发脉冲，通过Synapse Processing Units进行并行计算，并使用合并树将结果聚合至统一的Neuron Unit；2）软件框架：开发了划分与调度框架，在满足内存约束的前提下进行映射与启发式调度，最大化吞吐量。

Result: 在MNIST数据集上实现93.44%的准确率，延迟为149 μs，能耗为0.025 mJ/图，能效优于现有FPGA加速器5.6倍，延迟降低47.6%；在Spiking Heidelberg数据集上实现71.82%的准确率。

Conclusion: SupraSNN成功通过软硬件协同设计实现了突触级并行，在降低延迟和提升能效方面优于先前的FPGA SNN加速器，为高效部署大规模并行SNN提供了有效的架构方案。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a brain-inspired path toward highly efficient computation, but their practical deployment is constrained by the challenge of managing and executing their massive parallelism on physical hardware. This problem mirrors the historical challenge in processor design of moving beyond serial execution, a barrier broken by superscalar architectures that dispatch multiple instructions to parallel functional units. Drawing inspiration from this paradigm, we introduce a hardware-software co-design framework that treats synaptic events as parallelizable micro-operations. We present SupraSNN, a superscalar-inspired architecture that achieves high synapse-level parallelism by physically decoupling synaptic and neuronal computations. Within this architecture, a Multi-Cast Tree routes spike data to multiple parallel Synapse Processing Units serve as the computational pipelines, while a Merge Tree consolidates distributed results for processing by a unified Neuron Unit--deliberately centralizing complex neuron state dynamics to mitigate hardware overhead and resource duplication. The efficacy of this architecture is enabled by a sophisticated partitioning and scheduling framework that first maps the SNN onto hardware respecting memory constraints, then heuristic scheduling determines the synaptic execution order, maximizing throughput and resource utilization. Implementing a feedforward SNN trained on MNIST (93.44% accuracy), SupraSNN achieves 149 $μs$ inference latency and 0.025 mJ per image (0.276 nJ per synapse) on the Xilinx Zynq XC7Z020 FPGA--delivering 47.6% lower latency and 5.6$\times$ better energy efficiency than prior FPGA-based SNN accelerators. Beyond vision tasks, a recurrent SNN on the Spiking Heidelberg Dataset (71.82% accuracy) achieves 1.41 ms latency and 0.77 mJ per sample on XC7Z030.

</details>


### [358] [ReSCom: A Reconfigurable Spiking Neural Network Accelerator Using Stochastic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13560)
*Ali Alipour Fereidani,Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机计算的SNN加速器ReSCom，通过混合随机算术设计与统一神经元架构，在保障推理稳定性的前提下，显著提升了能效并实现了计算资源的动态可配置性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN具备低功耗潜力，但传统的硬件实现成本高昂，且直接应用非受控的近似算术会导致递归状态更新不稳定。

Method: 提出了一种自配置SNN加速器架构ReSCom；核心策略是利用随机计算（Stochastic Computing）进行神经元动力学中的乘法运算，同时保留定点加减法以确保稳定性；设计了统一的神经元模块，支持IF、LIF和Synaptic模型。

Result: 在Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA上进行MNIST实验，达到92.80%的分类精度，单张图像能耗仅为0.05 mJ，且支持通过动态调节随机比特流长度来实现精度、时延和能耗的灵活权衡。

Conclusion: ReSCom证明了随机计算在SNN硬件加速中的有效性，通过混合精度设计实现了高能效与灵活的精度-时延-能耗权衡。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) provide an attractive framework for energy-efficient inference due to their event-driven computation and biologically inspired dynamics. However, efficient hardware realization of SNNs remains challenging because neuronal computations incur significant power and area costs, and uncontrolled approximate arithmetic can destabilize recurrent state updates when precision is not properly managed. To address these challenges, this paper presents ReSCom, a reconfigurable SNN accelerator that leverages stochastic computing to reduce hardware complexity while maintaining stable inference. The proposed architecture employs stochastic arithmetic for multiplication operations in neuron dynamics, while preserving exact fixed-point addition/subtraction operations. This stochastic strategy enables runtime trade-offs between accuracy, latency, and energy consumption. A unified reconfigurable neuron design supports Integrate-and-Fire (IF), Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF), and Synaptic neuron models within a single hardware framework. Experimental results for MNIST inference on a Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA show that ReSCom achieves $92.80\%$ classification accuracy while consuming just $0.05~\mathrm{mJ}$ of operational energy per image at $100~\mathrm{MHz}$, outperforming the energy efficiency of recent state-of-the-art implementations. Furthermore, managing the stochastic bit-stream length allows explicit, dynamic control over accuracy-latency-energy trade-offs to meet target application constraints.

</details>

### [359] [Beyond Virtual Delay: Improving Packet Delay Bound in Network Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13631)
*Yuming Jiang*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本论文指出了网络演算中基于虚拟延迟的经典界限在处理分组延迟时的保守性，并推导出了一个更精确的新型分组延迟上界，在TSN等场景下表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的网络演算延迟界限依赖于虚拟延迟（virtual delay）的概念，在应用于分组延迟分析时存在固有的保守性（即经典界限往往过高）。因此，研究者旨在寻求一种更精确的分组延迟分析方法。

Method: 作者首先分析了分组延迟与虚拟延迟的关系，证明了分组延迟始终被虚拟延迟上界所覆盖。随后，在无需额外假设的情况下，推导出了一个新的分组延迟紧凑上界，并将其应用于漏桶流量约束与速率-时延（rate-latency）服务曲线模型。

Result: 理论推导显示，新提出的延迟上界在漏桶及速率-时延服务模型中严格优于经典界限。在时间敏感网络（TSN）案例研究中，实验结果证实了该方法能提供更紧凑、更准确的延迟估计。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种新的分组延迟上界，克服了经典网络演算中基于虚拟延迟方法所固有的保守性，并在实际系统模型及TSN场景中证明了其有效性。

Abstract: In network calculus, a fundamental result is the classical delay bound given by the horizontal deviation between the arrival and service curves. While widely used, the classical bound is derived from the notion of virtual delay. In this work, we first show that the maximum packet delay is always upper-bounded by the maximum virtual delay, revealing inherent conservatism when applying the virtual-delay-based bound to packet delay. Motivated by this insight, we revisit packet delay analysis and derive a new packet delay bound that requires no assumptions beyond the arrival and service curves. Specializing the new bound to a system with leaky-bucket arrival curve and rate-latency service curve shows strict improvement over the classical bound, which is further demonstrated through a case study in time-sensitive networking (TSN).

</details>
